## File: ./About.cs
```
﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    partial class About : Form
    {
        public About()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            this.Text = String.Format("About {0}", AssemblyTitle);
            this.labelProductName.Text = AssemblyProduct;
            this.labelVersion.Text = String.Format("Version {0}", AssemblyVersion);
            this.labelCopyright.Text = AssemblyCopyright;
            this.labelCompanyName.Text = AssemblyCompany;
            this.textBoxDescription.Text = AssemblyDescription;
        }

        #region Assembly Attribute Accessors

        public string AssemblyTitle
        {
            get
            {
                object[] attributes = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyTitleAttribute), false);
                if (attributes.Length > 0)
                {
                    AssemblyTitleAttribute titleAttribute = (AssemblyTitleAttribute)attributes[0];
                    if (titleAttribute.Title != "")
                    {
                        return titleAttribute.Title;
                    }
                }
                return System.IO.Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().CodeBase);
            }
        }

        public string AssemblyVersion
        {
            get
            {
                return Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetName().Version.ToString();
            }
        }

        public string AssemblyDescription
        {
            get
            {
                object[] attributes = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyDescriptionAttribute), false);
                if (attributes.Length == 0)
                {
                    return "";
                }
                return ((AssemblyDescriptionAttribute)attributes[0]).Description;
            }
        }

        public string AssemblyProduct
        {
            get
            {
                object[] attributes = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyProductAttribute), false);
                if (attributes.Length == 0)
                {
                    return "";
                }
                return ((AssemblyProductAttribute)attributes[0]).Product;
            }
        }

        public string AssemblyCopyright
        {
            get
            {
                object[] attributes = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyCopyrightAttribute), false);
                if (attributes.Length == 0)
                {
                    return "";
                }
                return ((AssemblyCopyrightAttribute)attributes[0]).Copyright;
            }
        }

        public string AssemblyCompany
        {
            get
            {
                object[] attributes = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyCompanyAttribute), false);
                if (attributes.Length == 0)
                {
                    return "";
                }
                return ((AssemblyCompanyAttribute)attributes[0]).Company;
            }
        }
        #endregion
    }
}
```

﻿using System;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class CalTable
    {
        public void SetTable(Int32 F, String filePath, ref CurretTable frm, CurrentResults frmR)
        {
            Int32 LastResultOfTeam1 = 0, LastResultOfTeam2 = 0, LastResultOfTeam3 = 0, LastResultOfTeam4 = 0,
                HittingGoals1 = 0, HittingGoals2 = 0, HittingGoals3 = 0, HittingGoals4 = 0,
                EatingGoals1 = 0, EatingGoals2 = 0, EatingGoals3 = 0, EatingGoals4 = 0;

            filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."));
            filePath = filePath + "t.txt";
            if ( F > 0)
            {
                String A = (new LoadSave()).Load(filePath);
                for (Int32 i = 0; i < (F - 1) * 8; i++)
                    A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
                String te1 = "", te2 = "";
                String S = "";
                while (te1 != frm.textBox1.Text)
                {
                    S = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
                    A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
                    while (S != ":*)")
                    {
                        te1 = (new LoadSave()).Getname(S);
                        if (te1 != frm.textBox1.Text)
                            S = S.Remove(0, S.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        else
                            break;
                    }
                }

                LastResultOfTeam1 = System.Convert.ToInt32((new LoadSave()).Getvalue(S));

                        SS[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text);

                        if (frmR.textBox8.Text == frm.textBox2.Text)
                            SS[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text);

            if (frmR.textBox1.Text == frm.textBox3.Text)
                SS[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox2.Text);
            else
                if (frmR.textBox4.Text == frm.textBox3.Text)
                    SS[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox3.Text);
                else
                    if (frmR.textBox5.Text == frm.textBox3.Text)
                        SS[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text);
                    else
                        if (frmR.textBox8.Text == frm.textBox3.Text)
                            SS[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text);

            if (frmR.textBox1.Text == frm.textBox4.Text)
                SS[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox2.Text);
            else
                if (frmR.textBox4.Text == frm.textBox4.Text)
                    SS[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox3.Text);
                else
                    if (frmR.textBox5.Text == frm.textBox4.Text)
                        SS[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text);
                    else
                        if (frmR.textBox8.Text == frm.textBox4.Text)
                            SS[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text);

            HittingGoals1 = HittingGoals1 + SS[0];
            HittingGoals2 = HittingGoals2 + SS[1];
            HittingGoals3 = HittingGoals3 + SS[2];
            HittingGoals4 = HittingGoals4 + SS[3];

            if (frmR.textBox1.Text == frm.textBox1.Text)
                EE[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox3.Text);
            else
                if (frmR.textBox4.Text == frm.textBox1.Text)
                    EE[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox2.Text);
                else
                    if (frmR.textBox5.Text == frm.textBox1.Text)
                        EE[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text);
                    else
                        if (frmR.textBox8.Text == frm.textBox1.Text)
                            EE[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text);

                EE[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox3.Text);

            if (frmR.textBox1.Text == frm.textBox1.Text)

                    QQ[0] = 3;

﻿using System;

    {

        public Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
        public Int32 j = 0;

        public float[] Team1Successproaility = new float[6];
        public float[] Team2Successproaility = new float[6];
        public float[] Team3Successproaility = new float[6];

        public float[] Team5Successproaility = new float[6];

        public String Team3 = "";
        public String Team4 = "";
        public String Team5 = "";

        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void Diagram_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics formGraphics = this.CreateGraphics();
            Font drawFont = new Font("Arial", 8);
            SolidBrush drawBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.Black);

            // Create point for upper-left corner of drawing.
            PointF drawPoint = new PointF(250.0F, 5.0F);
            Pen myPen = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Black);
            // Draw string to screen.
            formGraphics.DrawString(". نمودار پیشرفت گروه " + (j + 1).ToString(), drawFont, drawBrush, drawPoint);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 20, 20, 20, 410);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 10, 400, 400, 400);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 20, 20, 15, 25);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 20, 20, 25, 25);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 400, 400, 395, 395);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 400, 400, 395, 405);
            
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 15, 185, 25, 185);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 15, 30, 25, 30);

            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 70, 395, 70, 405);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 120, 395, 120, 405);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 170, 395, 170, 405);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 220, 395, 220, 405);

## File: ./Form1.cs
```
﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    public partial class Form1 : Form
    {
        public Form1()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void radioButton1_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void radioButton3_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void Form1_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void groupBox2_Enter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    Gropeumber = 2;
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        Gropeumber = 4;
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            Gropeumber = 8;

                Int32 i = 0;
                 Path pt = new Path();
                pt.ShowDialog();
                CurretTable frm = new CurretTable(pt.textBox1.Text);
                while (i < Gropeumber)
                {
                    NineNodeTree.Reset();
                    frm.ShowDialog();
                    NineNodeTree.Initate(i + 1, 4, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, frm);
                    LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
                    ldsv.SetTeamSuccessproaility(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team5Successproaility, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای جدول زیر :" + "\n" + frm.textBox1.Text + "          " + frm.textBox5.Text + "          " + frm.textBox9.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox2.Text + "          " + frm.textBox6.Text + "          " + frm.textBox10.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox3.Text + "          " + frm.textBox7.Text + "          " + frm.textBox11.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox4.Text + "          " + frm.textBox8.Text + "          " + frm.textBox12.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    if (Gropeumber == 2)
                    {
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                    }
                    else
                        if (Gropeumber == 4)
                        {
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                        }
                        else
                            if (Gropeumber == 8)
                            {
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                            }
                    i++;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n====================================================================\n";
                }
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
        }

        private void button2_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)

## File: ./Interpolate.cs
```
﻿/***********************************
 * Ramin Edjlal.CopyRigth 2015
 * Polynomial Interpolation.
 */
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class Interpolate
    {
        Double[,] D;
        Double[] F;
        Double[,] AS;
        public float[] Array(float[] ArrayInput, Int32 F)
        {
            float[] ArrayOutputA = new float[320];
            Double[] ArrayOutput;
            Int32 FF = (F - 1) * 50 + 20;
            ArrayOutput = Answer(ArrayInput[0], ArrayInput[1], ArrayInput[2], ArrayInput[3], ArrayInput[4], ArrayInput[5], ArrayInput[6], ArrayInput[7], F);
            for (Int32 ii = 20; ii < FF + 20; ii++)
                for (Int32 i = 0; i < F; i++)
                    ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] = ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] + (float)(ArrayOutput[i] * System.Math.Pow(ii, i));
            return ArrayOutputA;

        }
        public float[] ArrayA(float[] ArrayInput, Int32 F)
        {
            float[] ArrayOutputA = new float[320];
            Double[] ArrayOutput;
            Int32 FF = F * 50 + 20;
            ArrayOutput = Answer(ArrayInput[0], ArrayInput[1], ArrayInput[2], ArrayInput[3], ArrayInput[4], ArrayInput[5], ArrayInput[6], ArrayInput[7], F + 1);
            for (Int32 ii = 20; ii < FF + 20; ii++)
                for (Int32 i = 0; i < F; i++)
                    ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] = ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] + (float)(ArrayOutput[i] * System.Math.Pow(ii, i));
            return ArrayOutputA;

        }
        Double[] Answer(Double a0, Double a1, Double a2, Double a3, Double a4, Double a5, Double a6, Double a7, Int32 n)
        {
            Double[] Ans = new Double[n];
            /*D = new Double[3, 3];
            D[0,0]=2;
            D[0,1]=1;
            D[0,2]=7;
            D[1,0]=1;
            D[1,1]=2;
            D[1,2]=-5;
            D[2,0]=0;
            D[2,1]=4;
            D[2,2]=1;
            AS = AMinuseOne(D, 3);
             */
            D = new Double[n, n];
            F = new Double[n];
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                if (n == 1)
                    D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                else
                    if (n == 2)
                    {
                        D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                        D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                    }
                    else
                        if (n == 3)
                        {
                            D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                            D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                            D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                        }
                        else
                            if (n == 4)
                            {
                                D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                            }
                            else
                                if (n == 5)
                                {
                                    D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                    D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                    D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                    D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                                    D[4, i] = System.Math.Pow(220, i);
                                }
                                else
                                    if (n == 6)
                                    {
                                        D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                        D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                        D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                        D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                                        D[4, i] = System.Math.Pow(220, i);
                                        D[5, i] = System.Math.Pow(270, i);
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if (n == 7)
                                        {
                                            D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                            D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                            D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                            D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                                            D[4, i] = System.Math.Pow(220, i);
                                            D[5, i] = System.Math.Pow(270, i);
                                            D[6, i] = System.Math.Pow(320, i);
                                        }
            }
            if (n == 1)
            {
                F[0] = a0;                
            }
            else
                if (n == 2)
                {
                    F[0] = a0;
                    F[1] = a1;                    
                }
                else
                    if (n == 3)
                    {
                        F[0] = a0;
                        F[1] = a1;
                        F[2] = a2;                        
                    }
                    else
                        if (n == 4)
                        {
                            F[0] = a0;
                            F[1] = a1;
                            F[2] = a2;
                            F[3] = a3;                            
                        }
                        else
                            if (n == 5)
                            {
                                F[0] = a0;
                                F[1] = a1;
                                F[2] = a2;
                                F[3] = a3;
                                F[4] = a4;                                
                            }
                            else
                                if (n == 6)
                                {
                                    F[0] = a0;
                                    F[1] = a1;
                                    F[2] = a2;
                                    F[3] = a3;
                                    F[4] = a4;
                                    F[5] = a5;                                    
                                }
                                else
                                    if (n == 7)
                                    {
                                        F[0] = a0;
                                        F[1] = a1;
                                        F[2] = a2;
                                        F[3] = a3;
                                        F[4] = a4;
                                        F[5] = a5;
                                        F[6] = a6;                                        
                                    }
            D = AMinuseOne(D, n);

            for (Int32 i = 0; i < n; i++)
                for (Int32 j = 0; j < n; j++)
                    Ans[i] = Ans[i] + D[i, j] * F[j];
            return Ans;
        }
        public Double[,] AMinuseOne(Double[,] A, Int32 n)
        {
            Double[,] N = new Double[n, n];
            Double[,] Ast = new Double[n - 1, n - 1];
            for (Int32 ii = 0; ii < n; ii++)
                for (Int32 jj = 0; jj < n; jj++)
                    N[ii, jj] = System.Math.Pow(-1, ii + jj) * Det(AStar(A, n, ii, jj), n - 1);

            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
                for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++)
                {
                    Double AS = N[i, j];
                    N[i, j] = N[j, i];
                    N[j, i] = AS;
                }
            Double SAS = 1 / Det(A, n);
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
                for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
                    N[i, j] = SAS * N[i, j];
            return N;
        }
        Double Det(Double[,] A, Int32 n)
        {
            if (n == 0)
                return 0;
            if (n == 1)
                return A[0, 0];
            if (n == 2)
                return A[0, 0] * A[1, 1] - A[0, 1] * A[1, 0];
            Double AA = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
                AA = AA + A[0,i] * System.Math.Pow(-1, i) * Det(AStar(A, n, 0,i), n - 1);
            return AA;                   
        }
        Double[,] AStar(Double[,] A, Int32 n, Int32 ii, Int32 jj)
        {
            Double[,] Ast = new Double[n - 1, n - 1];
            Int32 ni = 0, nj = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                nj = 0;
                if ((i == ii))
                    i++;
                if (i == n)
                    break;
                for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
                {
                    if ((j != jj))
                    {
                        Ast[ni, nj] = A[i, j];
                        nj++;
                    }
                }
                ni++;

            }
            return Ast;
        }
    }
}

## File: ./LeageForm.cs

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    public partial class LeageForm : Form
    {
        public class Row
        {

            int len = 50;
            int Height = 25;
            public ComboBox c1 = new ComboBox();
            public ComboBox c2 = new ComboBox();
            public TextBox r1 = new TextBox();
            public TextBox r2 = new TextBox();
            public static int MaxWidth = 0;
            public Row(int number, Form frm, bool add = true)
            {
                c1.Location = new Point(MaxWidth + 5, number * Height + 5);
                r1.Location = new Point(MaxWidth + 5 + c1.Width + 5, number * Height + 5);
                r1.Size = new Size(r1.Size.Width / 2, Height);
                r2.Location = new Point(MaxWidth + 5 + c1.Width + 5 + r1.Width + 5, number * Height + 5);
                r2.Size = new Size(r2.Size.Width / 2, Height);
                c2.Location = new Point(MaxWidth + 5 + c1.Width + 5 + r1.Width + 5 + r2.Width + 5, number * Height + 5);
                if (add)
                {
                    frm.Controls.Add(c1);
                    frm.Controls.Add(c2);
                    frm.Controls.Add(r1);
                    frm.Controls.Add(r2);
                }
            }
            public void Dispose()
            {
                c1.Dispose();
                c2.Dispose();
                r1.Dispose();
                r2.Dispose();
            }
            public void Add(Row r)
            {
                c1.Text = r.c1.Text;
                c2.Text = r.c2.Text;
                r1.Text = r.r1.Text;
                r2.Text = r.r2.Text;
            }

        }
        public List<string[]> resultstring = new List<string[]>();
        List<Row> listrow = new List<Row>();
        List<string> items = new List<string>();
        string[] itemscopy = null;
        string path;
        List<List<Row>> listnextprevious = new List<List<Row>>();
        int indexnextprevious = 0;
        int[,] resultsTable;
        int indexR = 1;

        public LeageForm(LeatListTeamForm frm, string pa)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            path = pa.Substring(0, pa.LastIndexOf('\\'));
            items.Clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < frm.listteamname.Count; i++)
            {
                items.Add(frm.listteamname[i].c1.Text);
            }
            itemscopy = new string[items.Count];
            for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i++)
            {
                itemscopy[i] = items[i];
            }
            txtname.Text = (itemscopy.Length / 2).ToString();

        }

        private void txtname_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Row.MaxWidth = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
                listrow[i].Dispose();
            listrow.Clear();
            int max = 1;

            txtname.ReadOnly = true;
            for (int i = 0; i < System.Convert.ToInt32(txtname.Text); i++)
            {
                if (max != i / 10 + 1)
                    Row.MaxWidth += listrow[i - 1].c1.Width + listrow[i - 1].r1.Width + listrow[i - 1].r2.Width + listrow[i - 1].c2.Width + 25;

                Row r = new Row(i % 10 + 1, this);
                r.c1.TextChanged += new EventHandler(cbchanged);
                r.c2.TextChanged += new EventHandler(cbchanged);
                listrow.Add(r);
                max = i / 10 + 1;

            }
            InitiateComboBoxes(itemscopy);
        }
        public void InitiateComboBoxes(String[] r)
        {

            for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
            {
                listrow[i].c1.Items.Clear();
                listrow[i].c2.Items.Clear();
                listrow[i].c1.Items.AddRange(r);
                listrow[i].c2.Items.AddRange(r);
            }

        }

        private void LeageForm_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            listnextprevious = ReadAllresuls();

            if (listnextprevious.Count == 0)
            {
                btnnextresult.Enabled = false;
                btnpreviousresult.Enabled = false;

            }
            else
            {
                indexnextprevious = listnextprevious.Count - 1;

                btnnextresult.Enabled = false;
                btnpreviousresult.Enabled = true;

            }
            resultsTable = WriteResultTable(path);
            Printresult();
        }
        void cbchanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            bool Dif = true;
            string[] a = new string[listrow.Count * 2];
            for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
            {
                a[i * 2] = listrow[i].c1.Text;
                a[i * 2 + 1] = listrow[i].c2.Text;
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < a.Length; i++)
            {
                for (int j = 0; j < a.Length; j++)
                {
                    if (i == j)
                        continue;
                    if (a[i] == a[j] && a[i] != "")
                    {
                        ((ComboBox)sender).Text = "";
                    }

                }

            }
        }


        private void btnsave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SaveOneResult();
        }
        public void SaveOneResult()
        {
            string pathresult;
            if (path[path.Length - 1] != '\\')
                pathresult = path + "\\result";
            else
                pathresult = path + "result";
            if (!System.IO.Directory.Exists(pathresult))
                System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(pathresult);
            int index = 1;
            string filename = "R";
            while (System.IO.File.Exists(pathresult + "\\" + filename + index.ToString() + ".txt"))
            {
                index++;
            }
            string filenameresuktpath = pathresult + "\\" + filename + index.ToString() + ".txt";
            SaveResult(filenameresuktpath);

        }
        public void SaveResult(string filename)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
            {
                Row r = listrow[i];
                string a = "[" + r.c1.Text + ":" + r.r1.Text + "," + r.c2.Text + ":" + r.r2.Text + "]\n";
                if (!System.IO.File.Exists(filename))
                {
                    StreamWriter d = System.IO.File.CreateText(filename);
                    d.Write("");
                    d.Close();
                    System.IO.File.AppendAllText(filename, a);
                }
                else
                {
                    System.IO.File.AppendAllText(filename, a);
                }

            }

        }
        public List<List<Row>> ReadAllresuls()
        {
            List<List<Row>> r = new List<List<Row>>();
            string pathresult;
            if (path[path.Length - 1] != '\\')
                pathresult = path + "\\result";
            else
                pathresult = path + "result";
            if (!System.IO.Directory.Exists(pathresult))
                System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(pathresult);
            indexR = 1;

            string filename = "R";

            while (System.IO.File.Exists(pathresult + "\\" + filename + indexR.ToString() + ".txt"))
            {

                List<Row> rr = new List<Row>();

                string[] readtext = System.IO.File.ReadAllLines(pathresult + "\\" + filename + indexR.ToString() + ".txt");
                for (int i = 0; i < readtext.Length; i++)
                {
                    Row a = new Row((i - 1) % 10 + 1, this, false);
                    string s = readtext[i];
                    a.c1.Text = s.Substring(1, s.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                    a.r1.Text = s.Substring(s.IndexOf(":") + 1, s.IndexOf(",") - s.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                    a.c2.Text = s.Substring(s.IndexOf(",") + 1, s.LastIndexOf(":") - s.IndexOf(",") - 1);
                    a.r2.Text = s.Substring(s.LastIndexOf(":") + 1, s.LastIndexOf("]") - s.LastIndexOf(":") - 1);

                    rr.Add(a);
                    //a.Dispose();
                }
                r.Add(rr);
                indexR++;
            }
            return r;

        }
        void LoadOn()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
            {
                this.Controls.Add(listrow[i].c1);
                this.Controls.Add(listrow[i].r1);
                this.Controls.Add(listrow[i].c2);
                this.Controls.Add(listrow[i].r2);
            }

        }
        private void btnnextresult_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (btnnextresult.Enabled)
            {

                indexnextprevious++;
                for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
                {
                    listrow[i].Add(listnextprevious[indexnextprevious][i]);
                }


            }
            if (indexnextprevious >= 0)
                btnpreviousresult.Enabled = true;
            else
                btnpreviousresult.Enabled = false;

            if (indexnextprevious < listnextprevious.Count - 1)
                btnnextresult.Enabled = true;
            else
                btnnextresult.Enabled = false;
        }

        private void btnpreviousresult_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (btnpreviousresult.Enabled)
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < listrow.Count; i++)
                {
                    listrow[i].Add(listnextprevious[indexnextprevious][i]);
                }
                indexnextprevious--;

            }
            if (indexnextprevious >= 0)
                btnpreviousresult.Enabled = true;
            else
                btnpreviousresult.Enabled = false;

            if (indexnextprevious < listnextprevious.Count - 1)
                btnnextresult.Enabled = true;
            else
                btnnextresult.Enabled = false;
        }
        int IthInList(string name)
        {
            int Is = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < itemscopy.Length; i++)
                if (itemscopy[i] == name)
                    Is = i;
            return Is;

        }


        /// <summary>
        /// tableresut
        /// a[0] گل زده
        /// a[1] گل خورده
        /// a[2] امتیاز
        /// a[3] تعداد بازی
        /// a[4] تعداد برد
        /// a[5] تعداد باخت
        /// a[6] تعداد مساوی
        ///  
        ///  
        /// </summary>
        private int[,] WriteResultTable(string path)
        {
            int a = itemscopy.Length;
            int[,] tableofresut = new int[a, 7];
            string[] tableresultwritten = new string[a];
            for (int i = 0; i < listnextprevious.Count; i++)
            {

                for (int j = 0; j < listnextprevious[i].Count; j++)
                {
                    int s1 = IthInList(listnextprevious[i][j].c1.Text);
                    int s2 = IthInList(listnextprevious[i][j].c2.Text);
                    if (s1 < 0 || s2 < 0 || s1 > (itemscopy.Length - 1) || s2 > (itemscopy.Length - 1))
                        return tableofresut;
                    int g1 = System.Convert.ToInt32(listnextprevious[i][j].r1.Text);
                    int g2 = System.Convert.ToInt32(listnextprevious[i][j].r2.Text);
                    tableofresut[s1, 0] += g1;
                    tableofresut[s1, 1] += g2;

                    tableofresut[s2, 0] += g2;
                    tableofresut[s2, 1] += g1;

                    if (g1 == g2)
                    {
                        tableofresut[s1, 2]++;
                        tableofresut[s2, 2]++;
                        tableofresut[s1, 6]++;
                        tableofresut[s2, 6]++;
                    }
                    if (g1 > g2)
                    {
                        tableofresut[s1, 2] += 3;
                        tableofresut[s1, 4]++;
                        tableofresut[s2, 5]++;
                    }
                    if (g1 < g2)
                    {
                        tableofresut[s2, 2] += 3;
                        tableofresut[s2, 4]++;
                        tableofresut[s1, 5]++;
                    }

                    tableofresut[s1, 3]++;
                    tableofresut[s2, 3]++;
                }
            }
            string pathresult;
            if (path[path.Length - 1] != '\\')
                pathresult = path + "\\TR.txt";
            else
                pathresult = path + "TR.txt";
            bool Delete = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < a; i++)
            {
                string s = "[" + tableofresut[i, 0].ToString() + "," + tableofresut[i, 1].ToString() + ":" + tableofresut[i, 2].ToString() + "$" + tableofresut[i, 3] + "!" + tableofresut[i, 4].ToString() + "^" + tableofresut[i, 5].ToString() + "*" + tableofresut[i, 6].ToString() + "]\n";
                tableresultwritten[i] = s;
                if (!System.IO.File.Exists(pathresult))
                {
                    StreamWriter d = System.IO.File.CreateText(pathresult);
                    d.Write("");
                    d.Close();
                }
                else
                if (!Delete)
                {

                    System.IO.File.Delete(pathresult);
                    StreamWriter d = System.IO.File.CreateText(pathresult);
                    d.Write("");
                    d.Close();
                    Delete = true;
                }
                System.IO.File.AppendAllText(pathresult, tableresultwritten[i]);
            }
            return tableofresut;
        }
        private int[,] GetResultTableUntil(string path,int period)
        {
            int a = itemscopy.Length;
            int[,] tableofresut = new int[a, 7];
            string[] tableresultwritten = new string[a];
            for (int i = 0; i < period + 1; i++)
            {
                if (i > listnextprevious.Count - 1)
                    continue;

                for (int j = 0; j < listnextprevious[i].Count; j++)
                {
                    int s1 = IthInList(listnextprevious[i][j].c1.Text);
                    int s2 = IthInList(listnextprevious[i][j].c2.Text);
                    if (s1 < 0 || s2 < 0 || s1 > (itemscopy.Length - 1) || s2 > (itemscopy.Length - 1))
                        return tableofresut;
                    int g1 = System.Convert.ToInt32(listnextprevious[i][j].r1.Text);
                    int g2 = System.Convert.ToInt32(listnextprevious[i][j].r2.Text);
                    tableofresut[s1, 0] += g1;
                    tableofresut[s1, 1] += g2;

                    tableofresut[s2, 0] += g2;
                    tableofresut[s2, 1] += g1;

                    if (g1 == g2)
                    {
                        tableofresut[s1, 2]++;
                        tableofresut[s2, 2]++;
                        tableofresut[s1, 6]++;
                        tableofresut[s2, 6]++;
                    }
                    if (g1 > g2)
                    {
                        tableofresut[s1, 2] += 3;
                        tableofresut[s1, 4]++;
                        tableofresut[s2, 5]++;
                    }
                    if (g1 < g2)
                    {
                        tableofresut[s2, 2] += 3;
                        tableofresut[s2, 4]++;
                        tableofresut[s1, 5]++;
                    }

                    tableofresut[s1, 3]++;
                    tableofresut[s2, 3]++;
                }
            }
               return tableofresut;
        }
        private int TotalGoal()
        {
            int to = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < itemscopy.Length; i++)
            {
                to += resultsTable[i, 0];
                to += resultsTable[i, 1];

            }

            return to / 2;
        }
        private void Printresult()
        {
            if (itemscopy.Length > 0)
            {
                resultstring.Clear();
                int totalgoal = TotalGoal();


                for (int i = 0; i < indexR - 1; i++)
                {
                    int[] Golzadeh = new int[itemscopy.Length];
                    int[] GoleKhordeh = new int[itemscopy.Length];
                    int[] Emtize = new int[itemscopy.Length];
                    int[] Bord = new int[itemscopy.Length];
                    int[] Bakht = new int[itemscopy.Length];
                    int[] mosavi = new int[itemscopy.Length];
                    int Kolebaziha = resultsTable[0, 3];

                    resultsTable = GetResultTableUntil(path, i);

                    for (int j = 0; j < itemscopy.Length; j++)
                    {
                        Golzadeh[j] = resultsTable[j, 0];
                        GoleKhordeh[j] = resultsTable[j, 1];
                        Emtize[j] = resultsTable[j, 2];
                        Bord[j] = resultsTable[j, 4];
                        Bakht[j] = resultsTable[j, 5];
                        mosavi[j] = resultsTable[j, 6];


                    }
                    List<string> a = new List<string>();

                    All.ProbabilityMachesLearningAtamata t = new All.ProbabilityMachesLearningAtamata(itemscopy.Length, itemscopy.Length);


                    t.SetRewardPenaltyForProbabilityMaches(Golzadeh, GoleKhordeh, Emtize, Bord, mosavi, Bakht, totalgoal, Kolebaziha);

                    a.Add("Period ==== " + (i + 1).ToString() + "\n");

                  
                    for (int j = 0; j < itemscopy.Length; j++)
                    {
                        double r = t.LearningAlgorithmProbabilityMaches(j);
                        a.Add("Probability for " + itemscopy[j] + " Team is " + r.ToString() + "\n");
                    }
                    resultstring.Add(a.ToArray());
                }                
            }
            resultsTable = WriteResultTable(path);
        }
    }
}

## File: ./LeatListTeamForm.cs
```
﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using System.Xml.Linq;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    public partial class LeatListTeamForm : Form
    {
        public List<Row> listteamname = new List<Row>();
        int max = 1;
        string path = "";

        public class Row
        {
            int len = 50;
            int Height = 25;
            public Label c1 = new Label();
            public static int MaxWidth = 0;
            public Row(int number, Form frm, string te)
            {
                c1.AutoSize = true;
                c1.Location = new Point(MaxWidth + 5, number * Height + 55);
                c1.Text = te;
                frm.Controls.Add(c1);
            }
            public void Dispose()
            {
                c1.Dispose();
            }

        }
        public int Max()
        {
            int max = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < listteamname.Count; i++)
            {
                if (listteamname[i].c1.Width > max)
                    max = listteamname[i].c1.Width;
            }
            return max;
        }
        public LeatListTeamForm(string p)
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            path = p;
        }

        private void txtaddtolb_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            Row r = new Row(listteamname.Count % 10 + 1, this, txtteamname.Text);
            r.c1.MouseClick += new MouseEventHandler(lb_add);

            listteamname.Add(r);

            redraw();
        }
        public void lb_add(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < listteamname.Count; i++)
            {
                if (listteamname[i].c1.Equals((Label)(sender)))
                {
                    listteamname[i].Dispose();
                    listteamname.RemoveAt(i);
                }
            }
            redraw();

        }
        void redraw()
        {
            max = 0;
            Row.MaxWidth = 0;
            List<Row> r = new List<Row>();
            for (int i = 0; i < listteamname.Count; i++)
            {
                if (max != i / 10 + 1)
                    Row.MaxWidth += Max();
                Row rr = new Row(i % 10 + 1, this, listteamname[i].c1.Text);
                rr.c1.MouseClick += new MouseEventHandler(lb_add);

                r.Add(rr);
                listteamname[i].Dispose();

                max = i / 10 + 1;

            }
            listteamname.Clear();
            listteamname.AddRange(r);
        }

        private void btnsave_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SaveTeamsOnFile();
        }
        public void SaveTeamsOnFile()
        {
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path))
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < listteamname.Count; i++)
                {
                    string a = listteamname[i].c1.Text + "\n";
                    System.IO.File.AppendAllText(path, a);
                }
            }
        }

        private void LeatListTeamForm_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path))
            {
                string[] a = System.IO.File.ReadAllLines(path);
                for (int i = 0; i < a.Length; i++)
                {
                    Row r = new Row(i % 10 + 1, this, a[i]);
                    r.c1.MouseClick += new MouseEventHandler(lb_add);

                    listteamname.Add(r);

                }
                redraw();
            }
        }
    }
}

    {

        public String Team2Successproaility = "";

        public void SetTeamSuccessproaility(String Path, float a1, float a2, float a3, float a4, float a5, String b1, String b2, String b3, String b4, String b5)
        {
            Team1Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a1);
            Team2Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a2);
            Team3Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a3);
            Team4Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a4);

            Team2Successproaility = "";
            Team3Successproaility = "";
            Team4Successproaility = "";
            Team5Successproaility = "";
            Team1 = "";
            Team2 = "";
            Team3 = "";
            Team4 = "";
            Team5 = "";
            String A = Load(Path);
        }
        public String GetACollectio(ref String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
            A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
            return name;
        }
        public String Getname(String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(1, A.IndexOf(":"));
            return name;
        }
        public float Getvalue(String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(A.IndexOf(":") + 1, A.IndexOf("*") - (A.IndexOf(":") + 1));
            A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf("*") + 1);
            return (float)System.Convert.ToDouble(name);
        }
        public String Load(String filePath)
        {
            try
            {
                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();
                return text;
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {

            }
            return "";

        }
        public String Draw(String filePath, Int32 GropeNumber)
        {
            try
            {                
                Diagram frm1 = new Diagram();
                Diagram[] frm = new Diagram[GropeNumber];                
                System.Drawing.Pen[] myPen = new System.Drawing.Pen[4];
                System.Drawing.Graphics[] formGraphics = new System.Drawing.Graphics[GropeNumber];
                float[] last1 = new float[GropeNumber];
                float[] last2 = new float[GropeNumber];
                float[] last3 = new float[GropeNumber];
                float[] last4 = new float[GropeNumber];
                Int32[] ii = new Int32[GropeNumber];

                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();

                for (Int32 j = 0; j < 4; j++)
                    myPen[j] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Black);
                for (Int32 j = 0; j < GropeNumber; j++)
                {
                    ii[j] = 0;
                    last1[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    last2[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    last3[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    last4[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    frm[j] = new Diagram();
                    frm[j].Gropeumber = GropeNumber;
                    frm[j].j = j;
                    
                    frm[j].Show();

                    formGraphics[j] = frm[j].CreateGraphics();

                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 20, 20, 20, 410);

                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 170, 395, 170, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 220, 395, 220, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 270, 395, 270, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 320, 395, 320, 405);
                }
                myPen[0] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Red);
                myPen[1] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Blue);
                myPen[2] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Green);
                myPen[3] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Yellow);

                Int32 i = 0;
                Int32 jj = 0;
                while (text != "\r\n")
                {
                    frm1 = frm[i];
                    String first = GetACollectio(ref text);
                    frm1.Team1 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team1;
                    float value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team1Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[0], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last1[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    last1[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(1, first.IndexOf("*"));
                    frm1.Team2 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team2;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team2Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[1], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last2[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    last2[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(1, first.IndexOf("*"));
                    frm1.Team3 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team3;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team3Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[2], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last3[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));

        }

## File: ./NineNodeTree.cs
```
﻿//Programming by Ramin Edjlal.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class NineNodeTree
    {
        static NineNodeTree Node;
        //Next Nodes
        NineNodeTree a1;
        NineNodeTree a2;
        NineNodeTree a3;
        NineNodeTree a4;
        NineNodeTree a5;
        NineNodeTree a6;
        NineNodeTree a7;
        NineNodeTree a8;
        NineNodeTree a9;
        //NineNodeTree a10;

        NineNodeTree b;

        static String Team1 = "";
        static String Team2 = "";
        static String Team3 = "";
        static String Team4 = "";
        static String Team5 = "";
        static String UntilNowMath = "";
        static String UntilNowMathD = "";

        String Math1 = "";
        String Math2 = "";

        //Grope Number
        Int32 GropeID;
        //Node In Level Tree

        Int32 GreatestIndex()
        {

            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = Team1Score;
            Scores[1] = Team2Score;
            Scores[2] = Team3Score;
            Scores[3] = Team4Score;

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])
                    {
                        Int32 D = Scores[i];
                        Scores[i] = Scores[j];
                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex[3] - 1;

        }
        Int32 OneRemainToGreatestIndex()
        {
            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = Team1Score;
            Scores[1] = Team2Score;
            Scores[2] = Team3Score;
            Scores[3] = Team4Score;

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])
                    {
                        Int32 D = Scores[i];
                        Scores[i] = Scores[j];
                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex[2] - 1;
        }
        Int32 TwoRemainToGreatestIndex()
        {
            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = Team1Score;
            Scores[1] = Team2Score;

                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex[1] - 1;
        }
        void Probability()
        {

            Team1Successproaility = (float)Team1Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team2Successproaility = (float)Team2Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team3Successproaility = (float)Team3Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team4Successproaility = (float)Team4Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
        }
        Int32[] GreatestIndexCT(CurretTable frm)
        {

            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox5.Text);
            Scores[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox6.Text);
            Scores[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox7.Text);
            Scores[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox8.Text);

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])
                    {
                        Int32 D = Scores[i];
                        Scores[i] = Scores[j];
                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex;
        }
        void Success()
        {
            if ((this.a1 == null) && (this.a2 == null) && (this.a3 == null) && (this.a4 == null) && (this.a5 == null) && (this.a6 == null) && (this.a7 == null) && (this.a8 == null) && (this.a9 == null))
            {
                if (Team1Climb)
                {
                    Team1Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team2Climb)
                {
                    Team2Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team3Climb)
                {
                    Team3Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team4Climb)
                {
                    Team4Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                return;
            }
            if (this.a1 != null)
                this.a1.Success();
            if (this.a2 != null)
                this.a2.Success();
            if (this.a3 != null)
                this.a3.Success();
            if (this.a4 != null)
                this.a4.Success();
            if (this.a5 != null)
                this.a5.Success();
            if (this.a6 != null)
                this.a6.Success();
            if (this.a7 != null)
                this.a7.Success();
            if (this.a8 != null)
                this.a8.Success();
            if (this.a9 != null)
                this.a9.Success();
        }
        public NineNodeTree(Int32 GropeId, Int32 Leveltree, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {

            GropeID = new Int32();
            GropeID = GropeId;

            LevelTree = new Int32();
            LevelTree = (Leveltree + 1);

            //if (LevelTree == ((TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2))
            //Determine umber of Palyes level.
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
                return;
            //Initialaize Winer States.
            a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a2 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a3 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a4 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a5 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a6 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a7 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a8 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a9 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            //Making String
            this.a1.b = this;
            this.a2.b = this;
            this.a3.b = this;
            this.a4.b = this;
            this.a5.b = this;
            this.a6.b = this;
            this.a7.b = this;
            this.a8.b = this;
            this.a9.b = this;

        }

        {

            GropeID = new Int32();
            GropeID = GropeId;

            LevelTree = new Int32();
            LevelTree = (Leveltree + 1);
        }

        void FindState(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, ref CurretTable frm, ref NineNodeTree Dummy)
        {
            if ((System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox5.Text) == Team1Score)
                && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox6.Text) == Team2Score)
                    && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox7.Text) == Team3Score)
                     && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox8.Text) == Team4Score))
            {
                Dummy = this;
                return;
            }
            if (this.a1 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a2 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a3 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a4 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a5 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a6 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a7 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a8 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a9 == null)
                return;
            this.a1.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref  frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a2.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a3.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a4.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a5.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a6.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a7.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a8.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a9.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
        }

        //Basic Method
        static public void Initate(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, String team1, String team2, String team3, String team4, CurretTable frm)
        {
            Team1 = team1;
            Team2 = team2;
            Team3 = team3;
            Team4 = team4;
            Node = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, -1, TeamIdInGrope);
            if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope();
            }
            else
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope();
            }
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMathD = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath;
            Node.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.MatchesLeveling(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateEqualTeamSates(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Node.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            Dummy.Success();
            Node.Probability();
        }
        void MatchesLeveling(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 1);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 2);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 3);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 4);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 5);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 6);
        }
        void MatchesCreating(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, Int32 level)
        {
            //          String Math1 = "";
            //          String Math2 = "";
            //          static String UntilNowMath = "";
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
                return;
            if ((level-1) == LevelTree)
            {
                String Dummy = "";

                Dummy = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":", 0));
                this.a1.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a2.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a3.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a4.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a5.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a6.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a7.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a8.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a9.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                Dummy = Dummy.Remove(0, Dummy.IndexOf(",", 0) + 1);
                this.a1.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a2.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a3.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a4.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a5.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a6.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a7.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a8.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a9.Math2 = Dummy;
            } 
            this.a1.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a2.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a3.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a4.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a5.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a6.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a7.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a8.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a9.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
        }
        static NineNodeTree CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Int32 A = 1;
            Int32[] SA1 = { 1, 1, 1, 2, 3, 4 };
            Int32[] SA2 = { 2, 3, 4, 1, 1, 1 };
                
            while (A < 7)
            {
                Int32 S1 = SA1[A - 1];
                Int32 S2 = SA2[A - 1];
                
                if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 2)))
                {
                    String DummyS = "";
                    DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                    {
                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                        A++;

                    }
                }
                else
                    if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 3)))
                    {
                        String DummyS = "";
                        DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                        {
                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                            A++;

                        }
                    }
                    else
                        if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 4)))
                        {
                            String DummyS = "";
                            DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                            if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                            {
                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                A++;

                            }
                        }
                        else
                            if (((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 1)))
                            {
                                String DummyS = "";
                                DummyS = "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                                if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                {
                                    UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                    A++;

                                }
                            }
                            else
                                if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 1)))
                                {
                                    String DummyS = "";
                                    DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                    {
                                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                        A++;

                                    }
                                }
                                else
                                    if (((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 1)))
                                    {
                                        String DummyS = "";
                                        DummyS = "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                        {
                                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                            A++;

                                        }
                                    }
            }
            return Dummy;
        }
        void CalculateEqualTeamSates(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
            {
                if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
                {
                    if ((Team1Score == Team2Score) && (Team1Score == Team3Score) && (Team1Score == Team4Score))
                    {

                        this.a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                        this.a1.Team1Climb = true;
                        this.a1.Team2Climb = true;

                        this.a1.Math2 = Math2;

        static NineNodeTree CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Int32 A = 1;
            while (A < 9)
            {
                Int32 S1 = -1;
                Int32 S2 = -1;
                S1 = (new System.Random()).Next(1, 5);
                do
                {
                    S2 = (new System.Random()).Next(1, 5);

                } while (S1 == S2);

                if (((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 5)))
                {
                    String DummyS = "";
                    DummyS = "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                    {
                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                        A++;

                    }
                }

                else
                    if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 5)))
                    {
                        String DummyS = "";
                        DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                        {
                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                            A++;

                        }
                    }

                    else


                        if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 5)))
                        {
                            String DummyS = "";
                            DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                            if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                            {
                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                A++;

                            }
                        }

                        else


                            if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 2)) || ((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 5)))
                            {
                                String DummyS = "";
                                DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                                if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                {
                                    UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                    A++;

                                }
                            }
                            else


                                if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 4)))
                                {
                                    String DummyS = "";
                                    DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                    {
                                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                        A++;

                                    }
                                }

                                else
                                    if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 2)) || ((S1 == 5) && (S2 == 4)))
                                    {
                                        String DummyS = "";
                                        DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team5 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                        {
                                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                            A++;

                                        }
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if (((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 5) && (S2 == 1)))
                                        {
                                            String DummyS = "";
                                            DummyS = "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team5 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                            if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                            {
                                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                                A++;

                                            }
                                        }
                                        else
                                            if (((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 1)) || ((S1 == 5) && (S2 == 2)))
                                            {
                                                String DummyS = "";
                                                DummyS = "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                                DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team5 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
