        //Winer State Creating.
        void WinnerPalyers(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            if (LevelTree == ((TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2))
                return;
            NineNodeTree Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a1.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a1.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a2.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a2.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a3.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a3.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a4.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a4.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a5.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a5.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a6.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a6.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a7.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a7.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a8.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a8.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a9.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a9.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;


            this.a1.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a2.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a3.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a4.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a5.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a6.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a7.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a8.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a9.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
        }
        static NineNodeTree CalculateStates()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            if (!((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 2)))
            {
                if ((FirstGameS == -1) && (SecondGameS == -1))
                {
                    FirstGameS = 0;
                    SecondGameS = 0;
                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 0);
                }
                else
                    if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                    {
                        FirstGameS = 1;
                        SecondGameS = 0;
                        Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 0);
                    }
                    else
                        if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                        {
                            FirstGameS = 2;
                            SecondGameS = 0;
                            Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 0);
                        }
                        else
                            if ((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                            {
                                FirstGameS = 0;
                                SecondGameS = 1;
                                Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 1);
                            }
                            else
                                if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                {
                                    FirstGameS = 1;
                                    SecondGameS = 1;
                                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 1);
                                }
                                else
                                    if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                    {
                                        FirstGameS = 2;
                                        SecondGameS = 1;
                                        Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 1);
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if ((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                        {
                                            FirstGameS = 0;
                                            SecondGameS = 2;
                                            Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 2);
                                        }
                                        else
                                            if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 2))
                                            {
                                                FirstGameS = 1;
                                                SecondGameS = 2;
                                                Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 2);
                                            }
                                            else
                                                if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 2))
                                                {
                                                    FirstGameS = 2;
                                                    SecondGameS = 2;
                                                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 2);
                                                    FirstGameS = -1;
                                                    SecondGameS = -1;

                                                }
            }

            return Dummy;
        }
        void SetObjectsWE(Int32 a, Int32 b)
        {
            FirstGame = a;
            SecondGame = b;
        }
    }
}

## File: ./Probability.cs

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    public partial class Probability : Form
    {
        public Probability()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void radioButton1_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void radioButton3_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void Form1_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void groupBox2_Enter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    Gropeumber = 2;
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        Gropeumber = 4;
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            Gropeumber = 8;

                Int32 i = 0;
                Path pt = new Path();
                pt.ShowDialog();
                CurretTable frm = new CurretTable(pt.textBox1.Text);
                while (i < Gropeumber)
                {
                    NineNodeTree.Reset();
                    frm.label9.Text = "گروه " + (i + 1).ToString() + "را وارد کنید:";
                    frm.ShowDialog();                    
                    NineNodeTree.Initate(i + 1, 4, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, frm);
                    LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
                    ldsv.SetTeamSuccessproaility(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team5Successproaility, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای جدول زیر :" + "\n" + frm.textBox1.Text + "          " + frm.textBox5.Text + "          " + frm.textBox9.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox2.Text + "          " + frm.textBox6.Text + "          " + frm.textBox10.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox3.Text + "          " + frm.textBox7.Text + "          " + frm.textBox11.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox4.Text + "          " + frm.textBox8.Text + "          " + frm.textBox12.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    if (Gropeumber == 2)
                    {
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                    }
                    else
                        if (Gropeumber == 4)
                        {
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                        }
                        else
                            if (Gropeumber == 8)
                            {
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                            }
                    i++;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n====================================================================\n";
                }
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
        }

        private void button2_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            About frm = new About();
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void button3_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
            Path pt = new Path();
            pt.ShowDialog();
            if (radioButton4.Checked)
                ldsv.Draw(pt.textBox1.Text, 2);
            else
                if (radioButton6.Checked)
                    ldsv.Draw(pt.textBox1.Text, 4);
                else
                    if (radioButton10.Checked)
                        ldsv.Draw(pt.textBox1.Text, 8);
        }
    }
}

            Int32 LastResultOfTeam1 = 0, LastResultOfTeam2 = 0, LastResultOfTeam3 = 0, LastResultOfTeam4 = 0,
                HittingGoals1 = 0, HittingGoals2 = 0, HittingGoals3 = 0, HittingGoals4 = 0,
                EatingGoals1 = 0, EatingGoals2 = 0, EatingGoals3 = 0, EatingGoals4 = 0;

            {
                String A = (new LoadSave()).Load(filePath);
                for (Int32 i = 0; i < (F - 1) * 8; i++)
                    A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
                String te1 = "", te2 = "";
                String S = "";
                while (te1 != frm.textBox1.Text)
                {
                    S = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
                    A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
                    while (S != ":*)")
                    {
                        te1 = (new LoadSave()).Getname(S);
                        if (te1 != frm.textBox1.Text)
                            S = S.Remove(0, S.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        else
                            break;
                    }
                }

                LastResultOfTeam1 = System.Convert.ToInt32((new LoadSave()).Getvalue(S));

                            QQ[3] = 3;
                        else
                            if (System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text) == System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text))
                                QQ[3] = 1;
                    }
                    else
                        if (frmR.textBox8.Text == frm.textBox4.Text)
                        {
                            if (System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text) < System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text))
                                QQ[3] = 3;
                            else
                                if (System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox6.Text) == System.Convert.ToInt32(frmR.textBox7.Text))
                                    QQ[3] = 1;
                        }

            LastResultOfTeam1 = LastResultOfTeam1 + QQ[0];
            LastResultOfTeam2 = LastResultOfTeam2 + QQ[1];
            LastResultOfTeam3 = LastResultOfTeam3 + QQ[2];
            LastResultOfTeam4 = LastResultOfTeam4 + QQ[3];

            frm.textBox5.Text = System.Convert.ToString(LastResultOfTeam1);
            frm.textBox6.Text = System.Convert.ToString(LastResultOfTeam2);
            frm.textBox7.Text = System.Convert.ToString(LastResultOfTeam3);
            frm.textBox8.Text = System.Convert.ToString(LastResultOfTeam4);

            frm.textBox9.Text = System.Convert.ToString(HittingGoals1);
            frm.textBox10.Text = System.Convert.ToString(HittingGoals2);
            frm.textBox11.Text = System.Convert.ToString(HittingGoals3);
            frm.textBox12.Text = System.Convert.ToString(HittingGoals4);

            frm.textBox13.Text = System.Convert.ToString(EatingGoals1);
            frm.textBox14.Text = System.Convert.ToString(EatingGoals2);
            frm.textBox15.Text = System.Convert.ToString(EatingGoals3);
            frm.textBox16.Text = System.Convert.ToString(EatingGoals4);
         //   frm.ShowDialog();
        }
    }
}
```

        private void SetRegs(Regresion reg1, Regresion reg2)
        {
            if (Team1 == NineNodeTree.Team1)
                this.Team1Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility;
            else
                if (Team2 == NineNodeTree.Team1)
                    this.Team2Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility;
                else
                    if (Team3 == NineNodeTree.Team1)
                        this.Team3Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility;
                    else
                        if (Team4 == NineNodeTree.Team1)
                            this.Team4Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility;

            if (Team1 == NineNodeTree.Team2)
                this.Team1Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility;
            else

                    this.Team2Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility;

                    else

                            this.Team4Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility;

            if (Team1 == NineNodeTree.Team3)
                this.Team1Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility;
            else
                if (Team2 == NineNodeTree.Team3)
                    this.Team2Successproaility[F] = NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility;
                else

                                    frm.Text = frm.Text + "بر طبق پیش بینی در گروه " + (j + 1).ToString() + "\r\n"
            + "در دور " + iii.ToString() + " تیم " + reg1.Team1 + "با ،تیم " + reg1.Team2 + "مساوی میکنند.\r\n";
                                    frm.Text = frm.Text + "بر طبق پیش بینی در گروه " + (j + 1).ToString() + "\r\n"
                                                + "در دور " + iii.ToString() + " تیم " + reg2.Team1 + "با ،تیم " + reg2.Team2 + "مساوی میکنند.\r\n";

                                    NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();





                                    reg1.Sort(ref reg2); String[] TeamA = new String[4];
                                    Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];

                                    TeamA[0] = reg1.Team1;
                                    TeamA[1] = reg1.Team2;
                                    TeamA[2] = reg2.Team1;
                                    TeamA[3] = reg2.Team2;
                                    Scores[0] = (Int32)reg1.Score[0];
                                    Scores[1] = (Int32)reg1.Score[1];
                                    Scores[2] = (Int32)reg2.Score[0];
                                    Scores[3] = (Int32)reg2.Score[1];


                                    N[j].FindState(j , 4, ref Dummy, iii, TeamA, Scores);
                                    Dummy.Success();
                                    N[j].Probability();
                                    SetRegsForAll(reg1, reg2, iii);
                                }

                        }
                    frm.Text = frm.Text + "============================================================\r\n";
                }
                //=============
                myPen1[0] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Red);
                myPen1[0].DashPattern = dashValues;

                    last3[iii] = (float)1 / (float)2;

            }

        float Max(float[] A, Int32 n)
        {
            float Maxz = A[0];
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < n; i++)
                if (Maxz < A[i])
                    Maxz = A[i];
            return Maxz;
        }
        float Min(float[] A, Int32 n)
        {
            float Minz = A[0];
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < n; i++)
                if (Minz > A[i])
                    Minz = A[i];
            return Minz;
        }
        Boolean Is()
        {
            if (Team1Successproaility[F] != 0)
                return false;
            if (Team3Successproaility[F] != 0)
                return false;
            if (Team3Successproaility[F] != 0)
                return false;
            if (Team4Successproaility[F] != 0)
                return false;
            return true;
        }
        Boolean IsForAll()
        {
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 6; i++)
            {
                if (Team1SuccessproailityRegAll[i] != 0)
                    return false;
                if (Team2SuccessproailityRegAll[i] != 0)
                    return false;
                if (Team3SuccessproailityRegAll[i] != 0)
                    return false;
                if (Team4SuccessproailityRegAll[i] != 0)
                    return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

﻿using System;

        private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    Gropeumber = 2;
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        Gropeumber = 4;
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            Gropeumber = 8;

                Int32 i = 0;
                CurretTable frm = new CurretTable();
                Path pt = new Path();
                pt.ShowDialog();                    
                while (i < Gropeumber)
                {
                    NineNodeTree.Reset();
                    frm.ShowDialog();
                    NineNodeTree.Initate(i + 1, 4, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, frm);
                    LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
                    ldsv.SetTeamSuccessproaility(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team5Successproaility, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای جدول زیر :" + "\n" + frm.textBox1.Text + "          " + frm.textBox5.Text + "          " + frm.textBox9.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox2.Text + "          " + frm.textBox6.Text + "          " + frm.textBox10.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox3.Text + "          " + frm.textBox7.Text + "          " + frm.textBox11.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox4.Text + "          " + frm.textBox8.Text + "          " + frm.textBox12.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    if (Gropeumber == 2)
                    {
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                    }
                    else
                        if (Gropeumber == 4)
                        {
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                        }
                        else
                            if (Gropeumber == 8)
                            {
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                            }
                    i++;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n====================================================================\n";
                }
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
        }

        private void button2_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)

## File: ./ProbabilityMach/Interpolate.cs

﻿/***********************************
 * Ramin Edjlal.CopyRigth 2015
 * Polynomial Interpolation.
 */
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class Interpolate
    {
        Double[,] D;
        Double[] F;
        Double[,] AS;
        public float[] Array(float[] ArrayInput, Int32 F)
        {
            float[] ArrayOutputA = new float[320];
            Double[] ArrayOutput;
            Int32 FF = (F - 1) * 50 + 20;
            ArrayOutput = Answer(ArrayInput[0], ArrayInput[1], ArrayInput[2], ArrayInput[3], ArrayInput[4], ArrayInput[5], ArrayInput[6], ArrayInput[7], F);
            for (Int32 ii = 20; ii < FF + 20; ii++)
                for (Int32 i = 0; i < F; i++)
                    ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] = ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] + (float)(ArrayOutput[i] * System.Math.Pow(ii, i));
            return ArrayOutputA;

        }
        public float[] ArrayA(float[] ArrayInput, Int32 F)
        {
            float[] ArrayOutputA = new float[320];
            Double[] ArrayOutput;
            Int32 FF = F * 50 + 20;
            ArrayOutput = Answer(ArrayInput[0], ArrayInput[1], ArrayInput[2], ArrayInput[3], ArrayInput[4], ArrayInput[5], ArrayInput[6], ArrayInput[7], F + 1);
            for (Int32 ii = 20; ii < FF + 20; ii++)
                for (Int32 i = 0; i < F; i++)
                    ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] = ArrayOutputA[ii - 20] + (float)(ArrayOutput[i] * System.Math.Pow(ii, i));
            return ArrayOutputA;

        }
        Double[] Answer(Double a0, Double a1, Double a2, Double a3, Double a4, Double a5, Double a6, Double a7, Int32 n)
        {
            Double[] Ans = new Double[n];
            /*D = new Double[3, 3];
            D[0,0]=2;
            D[0,1]=1;
            D[0,2]=7;
            D[1,0]=1;
            D[1,1]=2;
            D[1,2]=-5;
            D[2,0]=0;
            D[2,1]=4;
            D[2,2]=1;
            AS = AMinuseOne(D, 3);
             */
            D = new Double[n, n];
            F = new Double[n];
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                if (n == 1)
                    D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                else
                    if (n == 2)
                    {
                        D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                        D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                    }
                    else
                        if (n == 3)
                        {
                            D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                            D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                            D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                        }
                        else
                            if (n == 4)
                            {
                                D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                            }
                            else
                                if (n == 5)
                                {
                                    D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                    D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                    D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                    D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                                    D[4, i] = System.Math.Pow(220, i);
                                }
                                else
                                    if (n == 6)
                                    {
                                        D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                        D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                        D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                        D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                                        D[4, i] = System.Math.Pow(220, i);
                                        D[5, i] = System.Math.Pow(270, i);
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if (n == 7)
                                        {
                                            D[0, i] = System.Math.Pow(20, i);
                                            D[1, i] = System.Math.Pow(70, i);
                                            D[2, i] = System.Math.Pow(120, i);
                                            D[3, i] = System.Math.Pow(170, i);
                                            D[4, i] = System.Math.Pow(220, i);
                                            D[5, i] = System.Math.Pow(270, i);
                                            D[6, i] = System.Math.Pow(320, i);
                                        }
            }
            if (n == 1)
            {
                F[0] = a0;                
            }
            else
                if (n == 2)
                {
                    F[0] = a0;
                    F[1] = a1;                    
                }
                else
                    if (n == 3)
                    {
                        F[0] = a0;
                        F[1] = a1;
                        F[2] = a2;                        
                    }
                    else
                        if (n == 4)
                        {
                            F[0] = a0;
                            F[1] = a1;
                            F[2] = a2;
                            F[3] = a3;                            
                        }
                        else
                            if (n == 5)
                            {
                                F[0] = a0;
                                F[1] = a1;
                                F[2] = a2;
                                F[3] = a3;
                                F[4] = a4;                                
                            }
                            else
                                if (n == 6)
                                {
                                    F[0] = a0;
                                    F[1] = a1;
                                    F[2] = a2;
                                    F[3] = a3;
                                    F[4] = a4;
                                    F[5] = a5;                                    
                                }
                                else
                                    if (n == 7)
                                    {
                                        F[0] = a0;
                                        F[1] = a1;
                                        F[2] = a2;
                                        F[3] = a3;
                                        F[4] = a4;
                                        F[5] = a5;
                                        F[6] = a6;                                        
                                    }
            D = AMinuseOne(D, n);

            for (Int32 i = 0; i < n; i++)
                for (Int32 j = 0; j < n; j++)
                    Ans[i] = Ans[i] + D[i, j] * F[j];
            return Ans;
        }
        public Double[,] AMinuseOne(Double[,] A, Int32 n)
        {
            Double[,] N = new Double[n, n];
            Double[,] Ast = new Double[n - 1, n - 1];
            for (Int32 ii = 0; ii < n; ii++)
                for (Int32 jj = 0; jj < n; jj++)
                    N[ii, jj] = System.Math.Pow(-1, ii + jj) * Det(AStar(A, n, ii, jj), n - 1);

            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
                for (int j = i + 1; j < n; j++)
                {
                    Double AS = N[i, j];
                    N[i, j] = N[j, i];
                    N[j, i] = AS;
                }
            Double SAS = 1 / Det(A, n);
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
                for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
                    N[i, j] = SAS * N[i, j];
            return N;
        }
        Double Det(Double[,] A, Int32 n)
        {
            if (n == 0)
                return 0;
            if (n == 1)
                return A[0, 0];
            if (n == 2)
                return A[0, 0] * A[1, 1] - A[0, 1] * A[1, 0];
            Double AA = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
                AA = AA + A[0,i] * System.Math.Pow(-1, i) * Det(AStar(A, n, 0,i), n - 1);
            return AA;                   
        }
        Double[,] AStar(Double[,] A, Int32 n, Int32 ii, Int32 jj)
        {
            Double[,] Ast = new Double[n - 1, n - 1];
            Int32 ni = 0, nj = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                nj = 0;
                if ((i == ii))
                    i++;
                if (i == n)
                    break;
                for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
                {
                    if ((j != jj))
                    {
                        Ast[ni, nj] = A[i, j];
                        nj++;
                    }
                }
                ni++;

            }
            return Ast;
        }
    }
}

        public String GetACollectio(ref String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
            A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
            return name;
        }
        public String Getname(String A)
        {
            if (A[0] == '\r')
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf("("));
            if (A[0] == '(')
                A = A.Remove(0, 1);
            String name = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
            return name;
        }
        public float Getvalue(String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(A.IndexOf(":") + 1, A.IndexOf("*") - (A.IndexOf(":") + 1));
            A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf("*") + 1);
            return (float)System.Convert.ToDouble(name);
        }
        public String Load(String filePath)
        {
            try
            {
                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();
                return text;
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {

            }
            return "";

        }
        public String Draw(System.Windows.Forms.RichTextBox frmP,String filePath, Int32 GropeNumber, Boolean RegResioForAllStates)
        {

                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[1], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last2[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    formGraphics[i].FillEllipse(MyBrush[1], (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 15, 395 - (value * 380), 10, 10);
                    formGraphics[i].DrawString(frm1.Team2, drawFont, MyBrush[1], new PointF(150.0F, 420.0F));
                    last2[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(0, first.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                    frm1.Team3 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team3;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team3Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[2], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last3[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    formGraphics[i].FillEllipse(MyBrush[2], (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 15, 395 - (value * 380), 10, 10);
                    formGraphics[i].DrawString(frm1.Team3, drawFont, MyBrush[2], new PointF(250.0F, 420.0F));
                    last3[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(0, first.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                    frm1.Team4 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team4;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team4Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[3], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last4[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    formGraphics[i].FillEllipse(MyBrush[3], (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 15, 395 - (value * 380), 10, 10);
                    formGraphics[i].DrawString(frm1.Team4, drawFont, MyBrush[3], new PointF(350.0F, 420.0F));
                    last4[i] = value;
                    ii[i]++;
                    i++;

                    if (i == GropeNumber)
                    {
                        jj++;
                        i = 0;
                    }
                    frm1.F++;
                }
                for (int j = 0; j < GropeNumber; j++)
                {
                    //Graphics g = frm[j].pictureBox1.CreateGraphics();
                    //Bitmap bitMap = Bitmap.FromHbitmap(g.GetHdc());
                    //bitMap.Save("C:\\Users\\Public\\" + j.ToString() + ".jpg", System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Jpeg);                    
                    formGraphics[j].Flush();
                    formGraphics[j].Dispose();
                }
                myPen[0].Dispose();
                myPen[1].Dispose();
                myPen[2].Dispose();
                myPen[3].Dispose();
                
                System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(".احتمالهای دور بعدی را به صورت پیش بینی برای تغییر آن به صورت خط های نقطه چین و تقریبی می بینید ");
                
                return text;
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
            return "";

        Int32 GreatestIndex()
        {

            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = Team1Score;
            Scores[1] = Team2Score;
            Scores[2] = Team3Score;
            Scores[3] = Team4Score;

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])
                    {
                        Int32 D = Scores[i];
                        Scores[i] = Scores[j];
                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex[3] - 1;

        }
        Int32 OneRemainToGreatestIndex()
        {
            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];

            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;

        public void Probability()
        {
            TotalSuccess /= 2;
            Team1Successproaility = (float)Team1Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team2Successproaility = (float)Team2Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team3Successproaility = (float)Team3Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team4Successproaility = (float)Team4Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
        }
        Int32[] GreatestIndexCT(CurretTable frm)
        {

            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox5.Text);
            Scores[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox6.Text);
            Scores[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox7.Text);
            Scores[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox8.Text);

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])
                    {
                        Int32 D = Scores[i];
                        Scores[i] = Scores[j];
                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex;
        }
        public void Success()
        {
            if ((this.a1 == null) && (this.a2 == null) && (this.a3 == null) && (this.a4 == null) && (this.a5 == null) && (this.a6 == null) && (this.a7 == null) && (this.a8 == null) && (this.a9 == null))
            {
                if (Team1Climb)
                {
                    Team1Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team2Climb)
                {
                    Team2Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team3Climb)
                {
                    Team3Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team4Climb)
                {
                    Team4Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                return;
            }
            if (this.a1 != null)
                this.a1.Success();
            if (this.a2 != null)
                this.a2.Success();
            if (this.a3 != null)
                this.a3.Success();
            if (this.a4 != null)
                this.a4.Success();
            if (this.a5 != null)
                this.a5.Success();
            if (this.a6 != null)
                this.a6.Success();
            if (this.a7 != null)
                this.a7.Success();
            if (this.a8 != null)
                this.a8.Success();
            if (this.a9 != null)
                this.a9.Success();
        }
        public NineNodeTree(Int32 GropeId, Int32 Leveltree, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {

            GropeID = new Int32();
            GropeID = GropeId;

            LevelTree = new Int32();
            LevelTree = (Leveltree + 1);

            //if (LevelTree == ((TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2))
            //Determine umber of Palyes level.
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
                return;
            //Initialaize Winer States.
            a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a2 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a3 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a4 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a5 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a6 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a7 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a8 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            a9 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope);
            //Making String
            this.a1.b = this;
            this.a2.b = this;
            this.a3.b = this;
            this.a4.b = this;
            this.a5.b = this;
            this.a6.b = this;
            this.a7.b = this;
            this.a8.b = this;
            this.a9.b = this;

        public NineNodeTree(Int32 GropeId, Int32 Leveltree, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, Int32 Dummy)

        void CalculateScores(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            //      String Math1 = "";
            //      String Math2 = "";

            if (Math1.Contains(NineNodeTree.Team1) && Math1.Contains(NineNodeTree.Team2))
            {
                if (NineNodeTree.Team1[0] == Math1[1])
                {
                    if (FirstGame == 0)
                    {
                        Team1Score = this.b.Team1Score + Equal;
                        Team2Score = this.b.Team2Score + Equal;
                    }
                    else
                        if (FirstGame == 1)
                        {
                            Team1Score = this.b.Team1Score + Win;
                            Team2Score = this.b.Team2Score;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            Team2Score = this.b.Team2Score + Win;
                            Team1Score = this.b.Team1Score;
                        }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (FirstGame == 0)
                    {
                        Team1Score = this.b.Team1Score + Equal;
                        Team2Score = this.b.Team2Score + Equal;
                    }
                    else
                        if (FirstGame == 1)
                        {
                            Team2Score = this.b.Team2Score + Win;
                            Team1Score = this.b.Team1Score;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            Team1Score = this.b.Team1Score + Win;
                            Team2Score = this.b.Team2Score;
                        }

                }

                                                                Team5Score = this.b.Team5Score;
                                                            }
                                                    }
                                                }
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
                return;
            this.a1.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a2.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a3.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a4.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a5.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a6.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a7.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a8.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a9.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
        }
        void FindState(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, ref CurretTable frm, ref NineNodeTree Dummy, Int32 PeriodNumber)
        {
            if ((System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox5.Text) == Team1Score)
                && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox6.Text) == Team2Score)
                    && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox7.Text) == Team3Score)
                     && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox8.Text) == Team4Score)
                && (PeriodNumber == LevelTree))
            {
                Dummy = this;
                return;
            }
            if (this.a1 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a2 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a3 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a4 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a5 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a6 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a7 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a8 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a9 == null)
                return;
            this.a1.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref  frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a2.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a3.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a4.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a5.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a6.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a7.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a8.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            this.a9.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
        }
        public void FindState(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, ref NineNodeTree Dummy, Int32 PeriodNumber, String[] TeamA, Int32[] Scores)
        {
            Regresion reg1 = new Regresion();
            Regresion reg2 = new Regresion();

            reg1.Score[0] = Team1Score;
            reg1.Score[1] = Team2Score;
            reg2.Score[0] = Team3Score;
            reg2.Score[1] = Team4Score;

            reg1.Team1 = Team1;
            reg1.Team2 = Team2;
            reg2.Team1 = Team3;
            reg2.Team2 = Team4;

            reg1.Sort(ref reg2);
            if (PeriodNumber == LevelTree)
            {
                if ((Scores[0] == reg1.Score[0])
                    && (Scores[1] == reg1.Score[1])
                        && (Scores[2] == reg2.Score[0])
                         && (Scores[3] == reg2.Score[1])
                    && (TeamA[0] == reg1.Team1)
                    && (TeamA[1] == reg1.Team2)
                    && (TeamA[2] == reg2.Team1)
                    && (TeamA[3] == reg2.Team2)
                    && (PeriodNumber == LevelTree))
                {
                    Dummy = this;
                    NineNodeTree.TeamA1 = reg1.Team1;
                    NineNodeTree.TeamA2 = reg1.Team2;
                    NineNodeTree.TeamA3 = reg2.Team1;
                    NineNodeTree.TeamA4 = reg2.Team2;
                    return;
                }
            }

            /*

            if (PeriodNumber == LevelTree)
            {
                if ((Scores[0] == Team1Score)
                    && (Scores[1] == Team2Score)
                        && (Scores[2] == Team3Score)
                         && (Scores[3] == Team4Score))
                {
                    Dummy = this;
                    return;
                }
            }
             */
            if (this.a1 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a2 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a3 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a4 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a5 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a6 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a7 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a8 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a9 == null)
                return;
            this.a1.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a2.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a3.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a4.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a5.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a6.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a7.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a8.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
            this.a9.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber, TeamA, Scores);
        }

        //Basic Method
        static public void Initate(Int32[] S1, Int32[] S2, Int32 PeriodNumber, Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, String team1, String team2, String team3, String team4, CurretTable frm)
        {
            Team1 = team1;
            Team2 = team2;
            Team3 = team3;
            Team4 = team4;
            Node = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, -1, TeamIdInGrope);
            if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope(S1, S2);
            }
            else
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope();
            }
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMathD = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath;
            Node.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.MatchesLeveling(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateEqualTeamSates(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Node.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy, PeriodNumber);
            Dummy.Success();
            Node.Probability();
        }
        static public void Initate(Int32[] S1, Int32[] S2, Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, String team1, String team2, String team3, String team4)
        {
            Team1 = team1;
            Team2 = team2;
            Team3 = team3;
            Team4 = team4;
            Node = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, -1, TeamIdInGrope);
            if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope(S1, S2);
            }
            else
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope();
            }
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMathD = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath;
            Node.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.MatchesLeveling(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateEqualTeamSates(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
        }
        void MatchesLeveling(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 1);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 2);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 3);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 4);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 5);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 6);
        }
        void MatchesCreating(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, Int32 level)
        {
            //          String Math1 = "";
            //          String Math2 = "";
            //          static String UntilNowMath = "";
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
                return;
            if ((level-1) == LevelTree)
            {
                String Dummy = "";

                Dummy = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":", 0));
                this.a1.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a2.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a3.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a4.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a5.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a6.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a7.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a8.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a9.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                Dummy = Dummy.Remove(0, Dummy.IndexOf(",", 0) + 1);
                this.a1.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a2.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a3.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a4.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a5.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a6.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a7.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a8.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a9.Math2 = Dummy;
            } 
            this.a1.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a2.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a3.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a4.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a5.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a6.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a7.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a8.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a9.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
        }
        public static NineNodeTree CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope(Int32[] SA1,Int32[] SA2)
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Int32 A = 1;
            //Int32[] SA1 = { 1, 1, 1, 3, 2, 4 };
            //Int32[] SA2 = { 3, 2, 4, 1, 1, 1 };
                
            while (A < 7)
            {
                Int32 S1 = SA1[A - 1];
                Int32 S2 = SA2[A - 1];

                if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 2)) || ((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 4)))
                {
                    String DummyS = "";
                    DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                    //if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                    {
                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                        A++;

                    }
                }
                else
                    if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 4)))
                    {
                        String DummyS = "";
                        DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                      //  if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                        {
                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                            A++;

                        }
                    }
                    else
                        if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 3)))
                        {
                            String DummyS = "";
                            DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                        //    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                            {
                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                A++;

                            }
                        }
                        else
                            if (((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 1)) || ((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 3)))
                            {
                                String DummyS = "";
                                DummyS = "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                          //      if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                {
                                    UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                    A++;

                                }
                            }
                            else
                                if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 1)) || ((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 2)))
                                {
                                    String DummyS = "";
                                    DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                            //        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                    {
                                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                        A++;

                                    }
                                }
                                else
                                    if (((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 1)) || ((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 2)))
                                    {
                                        String DummyS = "";
                                        DummyS = "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                              //          if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                        {
                                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                            A++;

                                        }
                                    }
            }
            return Dummy;
        }
        public void GetMatches(ref String Mat1, ref String Mat2, Int32 PeriodNumber)

                Mat2 = Math2;

            this.a9.GetMatches(ref Mat1, ref Mat2, PeriodNumber);        
        }
        void CalculateEqualTeamSates(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
            {
                if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
                {
                    if ((Team1Score == Team2Score) && (Team1Score == Team3Score) && (Team1Score == Team4Score))
                    {

                        this.a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                        this.a1.Team1Climb = true;
                        this.a1.Team2Climb = true;
                        this.a1.Math1 = Math1;
                        this.a1.Math2 = Math2;
                        this.a1.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;
                        this.a1.SecondGame = this.SecondGame;
                        this.a1.Team1Score = this.Team1Score;
                        this.a1.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;
                        this.a1.Team3Score = this.Team3Score;
                        this.a1.Team4Score = this.Team4Score;

                        this.a2.Team3Climb = true;

                        this.a2.Math2 = Math2;
                        this.a2.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;

                        this.a2.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;
                        this.a2.Team3Score = this.Team3Score;
                        this.a2.Team4Score = this.Team4Score;


                        this.a3 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);

                        this.a3.Team4Score = this.Team4Score;

                        this.a4.Math2 = Math2;

                        this.a6.b = this;


                        //EqaulTeamNumber = 4;
                    }
                    else
                        if ((Team1Score == Team2Score) && (Team1Score == Team3Score) && (Team1Score != Team4Score))
                        {
                            if (Team1Score > Team4Score)
                            {
                                this.a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                                this.a1.Team1Climb = true;

                                this.a3.Team3Climb = true;

                                this.a3.Math2 = Math2;

                                this.a1.b = this;
                                this.a2.b = this;
                                this.a3.b = this;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                this.a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                                this.a1.Team1Climb = true;
                                this.a1.Team4Climb = true;
                                this.a1.Math1 = Math1;

                                this.a2.Team2Climb = true;
                                this.a2.Team4Climb = true;
                                this.a2.Math1 = Math1;
                                this.a2.Math2 = Math2;
                                this.a2.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;
                                this.a2.SecondGame = this.SecondGame;
                                this.a2.Team1Score = this.Team1Score;
                                this.a2.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;

                                this.a3.Team3Climb = true;
                                this.a3.Team4Climb = true;

                                this.a3.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;
                                this.a3.Team3Score = this.Team3Score;
                                this.a3.Team4Score = this.Team4Score;

                                this.a1.b = this;
                                this.a2.b = this;
                                this.a3.b = this;

                            }
                            // EqaulTeamNumber = 3;
                        }
                        else
                            if ((Team1Score == Team2Score) && (Team1Score == Team4Score) && (Team1Score != Team3Score))
                            {
                                if (Team1Score > Team3Score)
                                {
                                    this.a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                                    this.a1.Team1Climb = true;
                                    this.a1.Team2Climb = true;
                                    this.a1.Math1 = Math1;
                                    this.a1.Math2 = Math2;
                                    this.a1.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;
                                    this.a1.SecondGame = this.SecondGame;
                                    this.a1.Team1Score = this.Team1Score;
                                    this.a1.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;
                                    this.a1.Team3Score = this.Team3Score;

        static NineNodeTree CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Int32 A = 1;
            while (A < 9)
            {
                Int32 S1 = -1;
                Int32 S2 = -1;
                S1 = (new System.Random()).Next(1, 5);
                do
                {
                    S2 = (new System.Random()).Next(1, 5);

                } while (S1 == S2);

                if (((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 5)))
                {
                    String DummyS = "";
                    DummyS = "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                    {
                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                        A++;

                    }
                }

                else
                    if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 5)))
                    {
                        String DummyS = "";
                        DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                        {
                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                            A++;

                        }
                    }

                    else


                        if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 5)))
                        {
                            String DummyS = "";
                            DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                            if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                            {
                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                A++;

                            }
                        }

                        else


                            if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 2)) || ((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 5)))
                            {
                                String DummyS = "";
                                DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                                if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                {
                                    UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                    A++;

                                }
                            }
                            else

                                {
                                    String DummyS = "";
                                    DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))

                                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                        A++;

                                    }
                                }

                                    {
                                        String DummyS = "";
                                        DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team5 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                        {
                                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                            A++;

                                        }
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if (((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 5) && (S2 == 1)))
                                        {
                                            String DummyS = "";
                                            DummyS = "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team5 + "-" + Team1 + ")";

        //Winer State Creating.
        void WinnerPalyers(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            if (LevelTree == ((TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2))
                return;
            NineNodeTree Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a1.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a1.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a2.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a2.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a3.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a3.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a4.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a4.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a5.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a5.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a6.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a6.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a7.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a7.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a8.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a8.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a9.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a9.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;


            this.a1.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a2.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a3.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a4.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a5.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a6.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a7.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a8.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a9.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
        }
        static NineNodeTree CalculateStates()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            if (!((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 2)))
            {
                if ((FirstGameS == -1) && (SecondGameS == -1))
                {
                    FirstGameS = 0;
                    SecondGameS = 0;
                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 0);
                }
                else
                    if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                    {
                        FirstGameS = 1;
                        SecondGameS = 0;
                        Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 0);
                    }
                    else
                        if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                        {
                            FirstGameS = 2;
                            SecondGameS = 0;
                            Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 0);
                        }
                        else
                            if ((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                            {
                                FirstGameS = 0;
                                SecondGameS = 1;
                                Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 1);
                            }
                            else
                                if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                {
                                    FirstGameS = 1;
                                    SecondGameS = 1;
                                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 1);
                                }
                                else
                                    if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                    {
                                        FirstGameS = 2;
                                        SecondGameS = 1;
                                        Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 1);
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if ((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                        {
                                            FirstGameS = 0;
                                            SecondGameS = 2;
                                            Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 2);
                                        }
                                        else
                                            if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 2))
                                            {
                                                FirstGameS = 1;
                                                SecondGameS = 2;
                                                Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 2);
                                            }
                                            else
                                                if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 2))
                                                {
                                                    FirstGameS = 2;
                                                    SecondGameS = 2;
                                                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 2);
                                                    FirstGameS = -1;
                                                    SecondGameS = -1;

                                                }
            }

            return Dummy;
        }
        void SetObjectsWE(Int32 a, Int32 b)
        {
            FirstGame = a;
            SecondGame = b;
        }
    }
}
