﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    public partial class Probability : Form
    {
        Path pt = new Path();
        String filePath1 = "";
        String filePath11 = "";
        public Probability()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void radioButton1_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void radioButton3_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void Form1_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void groupBox2_Enter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {

                if (this.comboBox1.Text == "")
                {
                    System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(".لطفا دور بازی ها را وارد کنید");
                    return;
                }

                Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    Gropeumber = 2;
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        Gropeumber = 4;
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            Gropeumber = 8;

                Int32 i = 0;


                pt.ShowDialog();
                CurretTable frm = new CurretTable(pt.textBox1.Text);
                CurrentResults frmR =new CurrentResults();
                Settings st = new Settings();
                
                if (radioButton3.Checked)
                {

                    while (i < Gropeumber)
                    {
                        NineNodeTree.Reset();


                        frm.label9.Text = "گروه " + (i + 1).ToString() + "را وارد کنید:";
                        st.LoadTeams(i + 1, pt.textBox1.Text);
                        st.LoadArrangedGame(i + 1, pt.textBox1.Text);
                        frm.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                        frm.textBox2.Text = st.Team2;
                        frm.textBox3.Text = st.Team3;
                        frm.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                        frm.ShowDialog();
                        NineNodeTree.Initate(st.S1, st.S2, System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text), i + 1, 4, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, frm);
                        LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessproaility(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team5Successproaility, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessState(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Success, NineNodeTree.Team2Success, NineNodeTree.Team3Success, NineNodeTree.Team4Success, NineNodeTree.Team5Success, NineNodeTree.TotalSuccess, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessHGoal(pt.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox9.Text, frm.textBox10.Text, frm.textBox11.Text, frm.textBox12.Text, "", frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessEGoal(pt.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox13.Text, frm.textBox14.Text, frm.textBox15.Text, frm.textBox16.Text, "", frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessTable(pt.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox5.Text, frm.textBox6.Text, frm.textBox7.Text, frm.textBox8.Text, "", frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای جدول زیر :" + "\n" + frm.textBox1.Text + "          " + frm.textBox5.Text + "          " + frm.textBox9.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                            "\n" + frm.textBox2.Text + "          " + frm.textBox6.Text + "          " + frm.textBox10.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text +
                            "\n" + frm.textBox3.Text + "          " + frm.textBox7.Text + "          " + frm.textBox11.Text + "          " + frm.textBox15.Text +
                            "\n" + frm.textBox4.Text + "          " + frm.textBox8.Text + "          " + frm.textBox12.Text + "          " + frm.textBox16.Text;
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Success + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم  " + frm.textBox2.Text + "  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Success + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم  " + frm.textBox3.Text + "  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Success + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم  " + frm.textBox4.Text + "  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Success + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "تعداد کل حالت های موفقیت این گروه  " + "  " + NineNodeTree.TotalSuccess + "است.";
                        if (Gropeumber == 2)
                        {
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        }
                        else
                            if (Gropeumber == 4)
                            {
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            }
                            else
                                if (Gropeumber == 8)
                                {
                                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                }
                        i++;
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n====================================================================\n";
                    }



                    CurretTable.ii = 0;
                    CurretTable.i = 0;
                }
                else
                {
                    while (i < Gropeumber)
                    {
                        NineNodeTree.Reset();

                        frm.label9.Text = "گروه " + (i + 1).ToString() + "را وارد کنید:";
                        st.LoadTeams(i + 1, pt.textBox1.Text);
                        st.LoadArrangedGame(i + 1, pt.textBox1.Text);
                        frm.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                        frm.textBox2.Text = st.Team2;
                        frm.textBox3.Text = st.Team3;
                        frm.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;
                        if (st.TeamxviaTeam1(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team2)
                        {
                            frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                            frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team2;

                            frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team3;
                            frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team4;
                        }
                        else
                            if (st.TeamxviaTeam1(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team3)
                            {
                                frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                                frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team3;

                                frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team2;
                                frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team4;
                            }
                            else
                                if (st.TeamxviaTeam1(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team4)
                                {
                                    frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                                    frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                                    frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team2;
                                    frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team3;
                                }
                                else
                                    if (st.TeamxviaTeam2(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team1)
                                    {
                                        frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                                        frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team2;

                                        frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team3;
                                        frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team4;
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if (st.TeamxviaTeam2(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team3)
                                        {
                                            frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team2;
                                            frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team3;

                                            frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team1;
                                            frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team4;
                                        }
                                        else
                                            if (st.TeamxviaTeam2(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team4)
                                            {
                                                frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team2;
                                                frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                                                frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team1;
                                                frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team3;
                                            }

                                            else
                                                if (st.TeamxviaTeam3(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team1)
                                                {
                                                    frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                                                    frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team3;

                                                    frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team2;
                                                    frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team4;
                                                }
                                                else
                                                    if (st.TeamxviaTeam3(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team2)
                                                    {
                                                        frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team2;
                                                        frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team3;

                                                        frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team1;
                                                        frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team4;
                                                    }
                                                    else
                                                        if (st.TeamxviaTeam3(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team4)
                                                        {
                                                            frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team3;
                                                            frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                                                            frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team1;
                                                            frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team2;
                                                        }
                                                        else
                                                            if (st.TeamxviaTeam4(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team1)
                                                            {
                                                                frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team1;
                                                                frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                                                                frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team2;
                                                                frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team3;
                                                            }
                                                            else
                                                                if (st.TeamxviaTeam4(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team2)
                                                                {
                                                                    frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team2;
                                                                    frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                                                                    frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team1;
                                                                    frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team3;
                                                                }
                                                                else
                                                                    if (st.TeamxviaTeam4(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 2) == st.Team3)
                                                                    {
                                                                        frmR.textBox1.Text = st.Team3;
                                                                        frmR.textBox4.Text = st.Team4;

                                                                        frmR.textBox5.Text = st.Team1;
                                                                        frmR.textBox8.Text = st.Team2;
                                                                    }


                        frmR.ShowDialog();
                        CalTable Calt = new CalTable();
                        Calt.SetTable(System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text) - 1, pt.textBox1.Text, ref frm, frmR);
                        NineNodeTree.Initate(st.S1, st.S2, System.Convert.ToInt32(this.comboBox1.Text), i + 1, 4, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, frm);
                        LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessproaility(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team5Successproaility, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessState(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Success, NineNodeTree.Team2Success, NineNodeTree.Team3Success, NineNodeTree.Team4Success, NineNodeTree.Team5Success, NineNodeTree.TotalSuccess, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessHGoal(pt.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox9.Text, frm.textBox10.Text, frm.textBox11.Text, frm.textBox12.Text, "", frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessEGoal(pt.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox13.Text, frm.textBox14.Text, frm.textBox15.Text, frm.textBox16.Text, "", frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessTable(pt.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox5.Text, frm.textBox6.Text, frm.textBox7.Text, frm.textBox8.Text, "", frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                        ldsv.SetTeamSuccessResult(pt.textBox1.Text, frmR.textBox2.Text, frmR.textBox3.Text, frmR.textBox6.Text, frmR.textBox7.Text, "", frmR.textBox1.Text, frmR.textBox4.Text, frmR.textBox5.Text, frmR.textBox8.Text, "");
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای جدول زیر :" + "\n" + frm.textBox1.Text + "          " + frm.textBox5.Text + "          " + frm.textBox9.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                            "\n" + frm.textBox2.Text + "          " + frm.textBox6.Text + "          " + frm.textBox10.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text +

                            "\n" + frm.textBox4.Text + "          " + frm.textBox8.Text + "          " + frm.textBox12.Text + "          " + frm.textBox16.Text;
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Success + "است.";

                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            }

                                }
                        i++;
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n====================================================================\n";
                    }




                    CurretTable.ii = 0;
                    CurretTable.i = 0;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
        }

        private void button12_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            pt.ShowDialog();

            String filePath11 = pt.textBox1.Text;
            filePath1 = filePath1.Remove(filePath1.IndexOf("."), 4);
            filePath1 = filePath1 + (2).ToString() + ".txt";
            filePath11 = filePath11.Remove(filePath11.IndexOf("."), 4);
            filePath11 = filePath11 + (2).ToString() + (2).ToString() + ".txt";

                this.button5.Enabled = true;

        private void button13_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            LoadSave ld = new LoadSave();
            String text = "";
            pt.ShowDialog();
            if (File.Exists(pt.textBox1.Text))
            {
                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(pt.textBox1.Text);
                text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();
            }
            String textS = "\r\n";

            String filePath = pt.textBox1.Text.Remove(pt.textBox1.Text.IndexOf("."));
            filePath = filePath + "s.txt";
            if (File.Exists(filePath))
            {
                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                textS = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();
            }

            Int32 ii = 1;
            Int32 i = 0;

            Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
            if (radioButton4.Checked)
                Gropeumber = 2;
            else
                if (radioButton6.Checked)
                    Gropeumber = 4;
                else
                    if (radioButton10.Checked)
                        Gropeumber = 8;
            if (text != "")
                while (text != "\r\n")
                {
                    String first = ld.GetACollectio(ref text);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(first);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + " احتمال صعود در دور  " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(first) * 100) + "%" + "است\r\n";

                    String firstS = ld.GetACollectio(ref textS);
                    if (firstS != "")
                    {
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(firstS);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم در دور   " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(firstS)) + " " + "است\r\n";
                    }
                    first = first.Remove(0, first.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(first);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + " احتمال صعود در دور  " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(first) * 100) + "%" + "است\r\n";

                    if (firstS != "")
                    {
                        firstS = firstS.Remove(0, firstS.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(firstS);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم در دور   " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(firstS)) + " " + "است\r\n";
                    }
                    first = first.Remove(0, first.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(first);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + " احتمال صعود در دور  " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(first) * 100) + "%" + "است\r\n";

                    if (firstS != "")
                    {
                        firstS = firstS.Remove(0, firstS.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(firstS);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم در دور   " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(firstS)) + " " + "است\r\n";
                    }
                    first = first.Remove(0, first.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(first);
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + " احتمال صعود در دور  " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(first) * 100) + "%" + "است\r\n";


                    if (firstS != "")
                    {
                        firstS = firstS.Remove(0, firstS.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای تیم " + ld.Getname(firstS);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "تعداد حالات موفقیت تیم در دور   " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(firstS)) + " " + "است\r\n";

                        firstS = firstS.Remove(0, firstS.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        firstS = firstS.Remove(0, firstS.IndexOf("*") + 1);
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "تعداد کل حالات موفقیت در دور  " + ii.ToString() + " ، " + System.Convert.ToString(ld.Getvalue(firstS)) + " " + "است\r\n";
                    }
                    i++;

                    if (i == Gropeumber)
                    {
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "============================================\r\n";
                        ii++;
                        i = 0;
                    }

                }
        }

        private void button3_Click_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Regresion.Reset();
            LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
            Path pt = new Path();
            pt.ShowDialog();
            if (radioButton7.Checked)
            {
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    ldsv.Draw(this.richTextBox1, pt.textBox1.Text, 2, false);
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        ldsv.Draw(this.richTextBox1, pt.textBox1.Text, 4, false);
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            ldsv.Draw(this.richTextBox1, pt.textBox1.Text, 8, false);
            }
            else
            {
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    ldsv.Draw(this.richTextBox1, pt.textBox1.Text, 2, true);
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        ldsv.Draw(this.richTextBox1, pt.textBox1.Text, 4, true);
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            ldsv.Draw(this.richTextBox1, pt.textBox1.Text, 8, true);            
            }
        
        }

﻿namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    partial class CurretTable
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Required designer variable.
        /// </summary>
        private System.ComponentModel.IContainer components = null;

        /// <summary>
        /// Clean up any resources being used.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="disposing">true if managed resources should be disposed; otherwise, false.</param>
        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (disposing && (components != null))
            {

﻿using System;

        private void Diagram_Paint(object sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            Graphics formGraphics = this.CreateGraphics();
            Font drawFont = new Font("Arial", 8);
            SolidBrush drawBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.Black);

            // Create point for upper-left corner of drawing.
            PointF drawPoint = new PointF(250.0F, 5.0F);
            Pen myPen = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Black);
            // Draw string to screen.
            formGraphics.DrawString(". نمودار پیشرفت گروه " + (j + 1).ToString(), drawFont, drawBrush, drawPoint);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 20, 20, 20, 410);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 10, 400, 400, 400);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 20, 20, 15, 25);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 20, 20, 25, 25);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 400, 400, 395, 395);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 400, 400, 395, 405);
            
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 15, 185, 25, 185);
            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 15, 30, 25, 30);

            formGraphics.DrawLine(myPen, 70, 395, 70, 405);

## File: ./ProbabilityMach/ProbabilityMach/LoadSave.cs
```
﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.IO;
using System.Drawing;
namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class LoadSave
    {
        public String Team1Successproaility = "";
        public String Team2Successproaility = "";
        public String Team3Successproaility = "";
        public String Team4Successproaility = "";
        public String Team5Successproaility = "";
        public String Team1 = "";
        public String Team2 = "";
        public String Team3 = "";
        public String Team4 = "";
        public String Team5 = "";
        public void SetTeamSuccessproaility(String Path, float a1, float a2, float a3, float a4, float a5, String b1, String b2, String b3, String b4, String b5)
        {
            Team1Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a1);
            Team2Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a2);
            Team3Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a3);
            Team4Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a4);
            Team5Successproaility = System.Convert.ToString(a5);
            Team1 = b1;
            Team2 = b2;
            Team3 = b3;
            Team4 = b4;
            Team5 = b5;
            this.Save(Path);
            Team1Successproaility = "";
            Team2Successproaility = "";
            Team3Successproaility = "";
            Team4Successproaility = "";
            Team5Successproaility = "";
            Team1 = "";
            Team2 = "";
            Team3 = "";
            Team4 = "";
            Team5 = "";
            String A = Load(Path);
        }
        public String GetACollectio(ref String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
            A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(")") + 1);
            return name;
        }
        public String Getname(String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(1, A.IndexOf(":"));
            return name;
        }
        public float Getvalue(String A)
        {
            String name = A.Substring(A.IndexOf(":") + 1, A.IndexOf("*") - (A.IndexOf(":") + 1));
            A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf("*") + 1);
            return (float)System.Convert.ToDouble(name);
        }
        public String Load(String filePath)
        {
            try
            {
                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();
                return text;
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {

            }
            return "";

        }
        public String Draw(String filePath, Int32 GropeNumber)
        {
            try
            {                
                Diagram frm1 = new Diagram();
                Diagram[] frm = new Diagram[GropeNumber];                
                System.Drawing.Pen[] myPen = new System.Drawing.Pen[4];
                System.Drawing.Graphics[] formGraphics = new System.Drawing.Graphics[GropeNumber];
                float[] last1 = new float[GropeNumber];
                float[] last2 = new float[GropeNumber];
                float[] last3 = new float[GropeNumber];
                float[] last4 = new float[GropeNumber];
                Int32[] ii = new Int32[GropeNumber];

                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();

                for (Int32 j = 0; j < 4; j++)
                    myPen[j] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Black);
                for (Int32 j = 0; j < GropeNumber; j++)
                {
                    ii[j] = 0;
                    last1[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    last2[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    last3[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    last4[j] = (float)1 / (float)4;
                    frm[j] = new Diagram();
                    frm[j].Gropeumber = GropeNumber;
                    frm[j].j = j;
                    
                    frm[j].Show();

                    formGraphics[j] = frm[j].CreateGraphics();
                    Font drawFont = new Font("Arial", 8);
                    SolidBrush drawBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.Black);

                    // Create point for upper-left corner of drawing.
                    PointF drawPoint = new PointF(250.0F, 5.0F);

                    // Draw string to screen.
                    formGraphics[j].DrawString(". نمودار پیشرفت گروه " + (j + 1).ToString(), drawFont, drawBrush, drawPoint);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 20, 20, 20, 410);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 10, 400, 400, 400);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 20, 20, 15, 25);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 20, 20, 25, 25);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 400, 400, 395, 395);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 400, 400, 395, 405);

                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 15, 185, 25, 185);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 15, 30, 25, 30);

                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 70, 395, 70, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 120, 395, 120, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 170, 395, 170, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 220, 395, 220, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 270, 395, 270, 405);
                    formGraphics[j].DrawLine(myPen[j], 320, 395, 320, 405);
                }
                myPen[0] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Red);
                myPen[1] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Blue);
                myPen[2] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Green);
                myPen[3] = new System.Drawing.Pen(System.Drawing.Color.Yellow);

                Int32 i = 0;
                Int32 jj = 0;
                while (text != "\r\n")
                {
                    frm1 = frm[i];
                    String first = GetACollectio(ref text);
                    frm1.Team1 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team1;
                    float value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team1Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[0], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last1[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    last1[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(1, first.IndexOf("*"));
                    frm1.Team2 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team2;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team2Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[1], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last2[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    last2[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(1, first.IndexOf("*"));
                    frm1.Team3 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team3;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team3Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[2], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last3[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    last3[i] = value;

                    first = first.Remove(1, first.IndexOf("*"));
                    frm1.Team4 = Getname(first);
                    frm1.Text = frm1.Text + " " + frm1.Team4;
                    value = Getvalue(first);
                    frm1.Team4Successproaility[jj] = value;
                    formGraphics[i] = frm1.CreateGraphics();
                    formGraphics[i].DrawLine(myPen[3], ii[i] * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(last4[i] * 380), (ii[i] + 1) * 50 + 20, 400 - (Int32)(value * 380));
                    last4[i] = value;
                    ii[i]++;
                    i++;
                    jj++;
                    if (i == GropeNumber)
                        i = 0;
                }
                for (int j = 0; j < GropeNumber; j++)
                {
                    //Graphics g = frm[j].pictureBox1.CreateGraphics();
                    //Bitmap bitMap = Bitmap.FromHbitmap(g.GetHdc());
                    //bitMap.Save("C:\\Users\\Public\\" + j.ToString() + ".jpg", System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Jpeg);                    
                    formGraphics[j].Flush();
                    formGraphics[j].Dispose();
                }
                myPen[0].Dispose();
                myPen[1].Dispose();
                myPen[2].Dispose();
                myPen[3].Dispose();
                
                return text;
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
            return "";

        }
        public void Save(String filePath)
        {
            String A = Load(filePath);
            StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(filePath);
            streamWriter.WriteLine(A + "(" +
                Team1 + ":" + Team1Successproaility + "*" +
            Team2 + ":" + Team2Successproaility + "*" +
            Team3 + ":" + Team3Successproaility + "*" +
            Team4 + ":" + Team4Successproaility + "*" +
            Team5 + ":" + Team5Successproaility + ")");
            streamWriter.Close();
        }
    }
}

        Int32 GreatestIndex()
        {

            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[0] = Team1Score;
            Scores[1] = Team2Score;
            Scores[2] = Team3Score;
            Scores[3] = Team4Score;

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])
                    {
                        Int32 D = Scores[i];
                        Scores[i] = Scores[j];
                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }
            }

            return ScoresIndex[3] - 1;

        }

        void Probability()
        {

            Team1Successproaility = (float)Team1Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team2Successproaility = (float)Team2Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team3Successproaility = (float)Team3Success / (float)TotalSuccess;
            Team4Successproaility = (float)Team4Success / (float)TotalSuccess;

        Int32[] GreatestIndexCT(CurretTable frm)
        {

            Int32[] Scores = new Int32[4];
            Int32[] ScoresIndex = new Int32[4];
            ScoresIndex[0] = 1;
            ScoresIndex[1] = 2;
            ScoresIndex[2] = 3;
            ScoresIndex[3] = 4;

            Scores[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox6.Text);
            Scores[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox7.Text);
            Scores[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox8.Text);

            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                for (int j = i; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (Scores[i] > Scores[j])

                        Scores[j] = D;
                        D = ScoresIndex[i];
                        ScoresIndex[i] = ScoresIndex[j];
                        ScoresIndex[j] = D;
                    }
                }

        void Success()
        {
            if ((this.a1 == null) && (this.a2 == null) && (this.a3 == null) && (this.a4 == null) && (this.a5 == null) && (this.a6 == null) && (this.a7 == null) && (this.a8 == null) && (this.a9 == null))
            {
                if (Team1Climb)
                {
                    Team1Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team2Climb)
                {
                    Team2Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team3Climb)
                {
                    Team3Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                if (Team4Climb)
                {
                    Team4Success++;
                    TotalSuccess++;
                }
                return;
            }
            if (this.a1 != null)
                this.a1.Success();
            if (this.a2 != null)
                this.a2.Success();
            if (this.a3 != null)
                this.a3.Success();
            if (this.a4 != null)

        {

            LevelTree = new Int32();

        void FindState(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, ref CurretTable frm, ref NineNodeTree Dummy)
        {
            if ((System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox5.Text) == Team1Score)
                && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox6.Text) == Team2Score)
                    && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox7.Text) == Team3Score)
                     && (System.Convert.ToInt32(frm.textBox8.Text) == Team4Score))
            {
                Dummy = this;
                return;
            }
            if (this.a1 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a2 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a3 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a4 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a5 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a6 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a7 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a8 == null)
                return;
            if (this.a9 == null)
                return;
            this.a1.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref  frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a2.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a3.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a4.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a5.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a6.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a7.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a8.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            this.a9.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
        }

        //Basic Method
        static public void Initate(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, String team1, String team2, String team3, String team4, CurretTable frm)
        {
            Team1 = team1;
            Team2 = team2;
            Team3 = team3;
            Team4 = team4;
            Node = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, -1, TeamIdInGrope);
            if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope();
            }
            else
            {
                NineNodeTree.CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope();
            }
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMathD = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath;
            Node.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.MatchesLeveling(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateScores(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            Node.CalculateEqualTeamSates(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Node.FindState(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, ref frm, ref Dummy);
            Dummy.Success();
            Node.Probability();
        }
        void MatchesLeveling(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 1);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 2);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 3);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 4);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 5);
            NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Remove(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":") + 1);
            this.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, 6);
        }
        void MatchesCreating(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope, Int32 level)
        {
            //          String Math1 = "";
            //          String Math2 = "";
            //          static String UntilNowMath = "";
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
                return;
            if ((level-1) == LevelTree)
            {
                String Dummy = "";

                Dummy = NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(":", 0));
                this.a1.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a2.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a3.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a4.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a5.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a6.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a7.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a8.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                this.a9.Math1 = Dummy.Substring(0, NineNodeTree.UntilNowMath.IndexOf(",", 0));
                Dummy = Dummy.Remove(0, Dummy.IndexOf(",", 0) + 1);
                this.a1.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a2.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a3.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a4.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a5.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a6.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a7.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a8.Math2 = Dummy;
                this.a9.Math2 = Dummy;
            } 
            this.a1.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a2.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a3.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a4.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a5.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a6.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a7.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a8.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
            this.a9.MatchesCreating(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope, level);
        }
        static NineNodeTree CalculteMathFourTeamInGrope()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Int32 A = 1;
            Int32[] SA1 = { 1, 1, 1, 2, 3, 4 };
            Int32[] SA2 = { 2, 3, 4, 1, 1, 1 };
                
            while (A < 7)
            {
                Int32 S1 = SA1[A - 1];
                Int32 S2 = SA2[A - 1];
                
                if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 2)))
                {
                    String DummyS = "";
                    DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                    {
                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                        A++;

                    }
                }
                else
                    if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 3)))
                    {
                        String DummyS = "";
                        DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                        {
                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                            A++;

                        }
                    }
                    else
                        if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 4)))
                        {
                            String DummyS = "";
                            DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                            if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                            {
                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                A++;

                            }
                        }
                        else
                            if (((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 1)))
                            {
                                String DummyS = "";
                                DummyS = "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                                if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                {
                                    UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                    A++;

                                }
                            }
                            else
                                if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 1)))
                                {
                                    String DummyS = "";
                                    DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                    {
                                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                        A++;

                                    }
                                }
                                else
                                    if (((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 1)))
                                    {
                                        String DummyS = "";
                                        DummyS = "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team1 + ")";
                                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team2 + ")";
                                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                                        {
                                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                            A++;

                                        }
                                    }
            }
            return Dummy;
        }
        void CalculateEqualTeamSates(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            if (LevelTree == (TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2)
            {
                if (TeamIdInGrope == 4)
                {
                    if ((Team1Score == Team2Score) && (Team1Score == Team3Score) && (Team1Score == Team4Score))
                    {

                        this.a1 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                        this.a1.Team1Climb = true;
                        this.a1.Team2Climb = true;
                        this.a1.Math1 = Math1;
                        this.a1.Math2 = Math2;
                        this.a1.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;
                        this.a1.SecondGame = this.SecondGame;
                        this.a1.Team1Score = this.Team1Score;
                        this.a1.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;
                        this.a1.Team3Score = this.Team3Score;
                        this.a1.Team4Score = this.Team4Score;


                        this.a2 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                        this.a2.Team1Climb = true;
                        this.a2.Team3Climb = true;
                        this.a2.Math1 = Math1;
                        this.a2.Math2 = Math2;
                        this.a2.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;
                        this.a2.SecondGame = this.SecondGame;
                        this.a2.Team1Score = this.Team1Score;
                        this.a2.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;
                        this.a2.Team3Score = this.Team3Score;
                        this.a2.Team4Score = this.Team4Score;


                        this.a3 = new NineNodeTree(GropeId, LevelTree, TeamIdInGrope, 0);
                        this.a3.Team1Climb = true;
                        this.a3.Team4Climb = true;
                        this.a3.Math1 = Math1;
                        this.a3.Math2 = Math2;
                        this.a3.FirstGame = this.FirstGame;
                        this.a3.SecondGame = this.SecondGame;
                        this.a3.Team1Score = this.Team1Score;
                        this.a3.Team2Score = this.Team2Score;

        static NineNodeTree CalculteMathFiveTeamInGrope()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            Int32 A = 1;
            while (A < 9)
            {
                Int32 S1 = -1;
                Int32 S2 = -1;
                S1 = (new System.Random()).Next(1, 5);
                do
                {
                    S2 = (new System.Random()).Next(1, 5);

                } while (S1 == S2);

                if (((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 3)) || ((S1 == 4) && (S2 == 5)))
                {
                    String DummyS = "";
                    DummyS = "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team3 + ")";
                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team4 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                    {
                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                        A++;

                    }
                }

                else
                    if (((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 5)))
                    {
                        String DummyS = "";
                        DummyS = "(" + Team3 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                        DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                        if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                        {
                            UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                            A++;

                        }
                    }

                    else


                        if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 4)) || ((S1 == 2) && (S2 == 5)))
                        {
                            String DummyS = "";
                            DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                            DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team5 + ")";
                            if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))
                            {
                                UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";
                                A++;

                            }
                        }

                        else


                            if (((S1 == 1) && (S2 == 2)) || ((S1 == 3) && (S2 == 5)))
                            {

                                DummyS = "(" + Team1 + "-" + Team2 + ")";

                                    String DummyS = "";

                                    DummyS = DummyS + ',' + "(" + Team2 + "-" + Team4 + ")";
                                    if (!UntilNowMath.Contains(DummyS))

                                        UntilNowMath = UntilNowMath + DummyS + ":";

        //Winer State Creating.
        void WinnerPalyers(Int32 GropeId, Int32 TeamIdInGrope)
        {
            if (LevelTree == ((TeamIdInGrope - 1) * 2))
                return;
            NineNodeTree Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a1.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a1.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a2.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a2.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a3.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a3.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a4.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a4.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a5.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a5.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a6.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a6.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a7.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a7.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a8.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a8.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;

            Dummy = NineNodeTree.CalculateStates();
            this.a9.FirstGame = Dummy.FirstGame;
            this.a9.SecondGame = Dummy.SecondGame;


            this.a1.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a2.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a3.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a4.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a5.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a6.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a7.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a8.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
            this.a9.WinnerPalyers(GropeId, TeamIdInGrope);
        }
        static NineNodeTree CalculateStates()
        {
            NineNodeTree Dummy = new NineNodeTree();
            if (!((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 2)))
            {
                if ((FirstGameS == -1) && (SecondGameS == -1))
                {
                    FirstGameS = 0;
                    SecondGameS = 0;
                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 0);
                }
                else
                    if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                    {
                        FirstGameS = 1;
                        SecondGameS = 0;
                        Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 0);
                    }
                    else
                        if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                        {
                            FirstGameS = 2;
                            SecondGameS = 0;
                            Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 0);
                        }
                        else
                            if ((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 0))
                            {
                                FirstGameS = 0;
                                SecondGameS = 1;
                                Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 1);
                            }
                            else
                                if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                {
                                    FirstGameS = 1;
                                    SecondGameS = 1;
                                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 1);
                                }
                                else
                                    if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                    {
                                        FirstGameS = 2;
                                        SecondGameS = 1;
                                        Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 1);
                                    }
                                    else
                                        if ((FirstGameS == 2) && (SecondGameS == 1))
                                        {
                                            FirstGameS = 0;
                                            SecondGameS = 2;
                                            Dummy.SetObjectsWE(0, 2);
                                        }
                                        else
                                            if ((FirstGameS == 0) && (SecondGameS == 2))
                                            {
                                                FirstGameS = 1;
                                                SecondGameS = 2;
                                                Dummy.SetObjectsWE(1, 2);
                                            }
                                            else
                                                if ((FirstGameS == 1) && (SecondGameS == 2))
                                                {
                                                    FirstGameS = 2;
                                                    SecondGameS = 2;
                                                    Dummy.SetObjectsWE(2, 2);
                                                    FirstGameS = -1;
                                                    SecondGameS = -1;

                                                }
            }

            return Dummy;
        }
        void SetObjectsWE(Int32 a, Int32 b)
        {
            FirstGame = a;
            SecondGame = b;
        }
    }
}

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    public partial class Probability : Form
    {
        public Probability()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void radioButton1_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void radioButton3_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void Form1_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void groupBox2_Enter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

        }

        private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                Int32 Gropeumber = 0;
                if (radioButton4.Checked)
                    Gropeumber = 2;
                else
                    if (radioButton6.Checked)
                        Gropeumber = 4;
                    else
                        if (radioButton10.Checked)
                            Gropeumber = 8;

                Int32 i = 0;
                CurretTable frm = new CurretTable();
                Path pt = new Path();
                pt.ShowDialog();                    
                while (i < Gropeumber)
                {
                    NineNodeTree.Reset();
                    frm.label9.Text = "گروه " + (i + 1).ToString() + "را وارد کنید:";
                    frm.ShowDialog();                    
                    NineNodeTree.Initate(i + 1, 4, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, frm);
                    LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
                    ldsv.SetTeamSuccessproaility(pt.textBox1.Text, NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility, NineNodeTree.Team5Successproaility, frm.textBox1.Text, frm.textBox2.Text, frm.textBox3.Text, frm.textBox4.Text, "");
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "برای جدول زیر :" + "\n" + frm.textBox1.Text + "          " + frm.textBox5.Text + "          " + frm.textBox9.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox2.Text + "          " + frm.textBox6.Text + "          " + frm.textBox10.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox3.Text + "          " + frm.textBox7.Text + "          " + frm.textBox11.Text + "          " + frm.textBox13.Text +
                        "\n" + frm.textBox4.Text + "          " + frm.textBox8.Text + "          " + frm.textBox12.Text + "          " + frm.textBox14.Text;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم  " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال صعود تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * 100 + "است.";
                    if (Gropeumber == 2)
                    {
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                        richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 1) * 100 + "است.";
                    }
                    else
                        if (Gropeumber == 4)
                        {
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                            richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 3) * 100 + "است.";
                        }
                        else
                            if (Gropeumber == 8)
                            {
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox1.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team1Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox2.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team2Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox3.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team3Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                                richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n" + "احتمال قهرمانی تیم " + frm.textBox4.Text + "%  " + NineNodeTree.Team4Successproaility * Math.Pow(0.5, 4) * 100 + "است.";
                            }
                    i++;
                    richTextBox1.Text = richTextBox1.Text + "\n====================================================================\n";
                }
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(t.ToString());
            }
        }

        private void button2_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            About frm = new About();
            frm.ShowDialog();
        }

        private void button3_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            LoadSave ldsv = new LoadSave();
            Path pt = new Path();
            pt.ShowDialog();
            if (radioButton4.Checked)
                ldsv.Draw(pt.textBox1.Text, 2);
            else
                if (radioButton6.Checked)
                    ldsv.Draw(pt.textBox1.Text, 4);
                else
                    if (radioButton10.Checked)
                        ldsv.Draw(pt.textBox1.Text, 8);
        }
    }
}

﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
/*****************************
 * Ramin Edjlal CopyRigth 2015
 * Setting.
 * Configuration Settings. 
 */
using System.Text;
using System.IO;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class Settings
    {
        public String Team1 = "";
        public String Team2 = "";
        public String Team3 = "";
        public String Team4 = "";
        String A = "";
        public Int32[] S1 = new Int32[6];
        public Int32[] S2 = new Int32[6];
        public String LoadTeams(Int32 I, String filePath)
        {
            try
            {
                filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."), 4);
                filePath = filePath + I.ToString() + ".txt";

                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();
                Team1 = text.Substring(1, text.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                text = text.Remove(1, text.IndexOf(":"));

                Team2 = text.Substring(1, text.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                text = text.Remove(1, text.IndexOf(":"));

                Team3 = text.Substring(1, text.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                text = text.Remove(1, text.IndexOf(":"));

                Team4 = text.Substring(1, text.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                text = text.Remove(1, text.IndexOf(":"));
                return text;
            }
            catch (Exception t)
            {

            }
            return "";

        }
        public String LoadArrangedGame(Int32 I, String filePath)
        {
            try
            {
                filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."), 4);
                filePath = filePath + I.ToString() + I.ToString() + ".txt";

                StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(filePath);
                String text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
                streamReader.Close();


                S1[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(text.IndexOf("{") + 1, text.IndexOf(",") - text.IndexOf("{") - 1));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);


                S1[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S1[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S1[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S1[4] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S1[5] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S2[0] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(text.IndexOf("{") + 1, text.IndexOf(",") - text.IndexOf("{") - 1));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);


                S2[1] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S2[2] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S2[3] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S2[4] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                S2[5] = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf(",")));
                text = text.Remove(0, text.IndexOf(",") + 1);

                return text;
            }

            catch (Exception t)
            {
            }
            return "";
        }
        public void SaveTeams(Int32 I, String filePath, String team1, String team2, String team3, String team4)
        {
            filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."), 4);
            filePath = filePath + I.ToString() + ".txt";

            StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(filePath);
            streamWriter.WriteLine("(" +
                team1 + ":" +
                team2 + ":" +
                team3 + ":" +
                team4 + ":" +
                ")");
            streamWriter.Close();
        }
        public void SaveArrangedGame(Int32 I, String filePath,String Set1,String Set2)
        {
            filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."), 4);
            filePath = filePath + I.ToString() + I.ToString() + ".txt";

            StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(filePath);
            streamWriter.WriteLine("(" +
                Set1 + ":" +
                Set2 + ":" +
                ")");
            streamWriter.Close();
        }
        public String TeamxviaTeam1(Int32 Period)
        {
            String Team = "";
            if (S1[Period + 1] == 1)
            {
                if (S2[Period + 1] == 2)
                    Team = Team2;
                else
                    if (S2[Period + 1] == 3)
                        Team = Team3;
                    else
                        if (S2[Period + 1] == 4)
                            Team = Team4;
            }
            return Team;
        }
        public String TeamxviaTeam2(Int32 Period)
        {
            String Team = "";
            if (S1[Period + 1] == 2)
            {
                if (S2[Period + 1] == 1)
                    Team = Team1;
                else
                    if (S2[Period + 1] == 3)
                        Team = Team3;
                    else
                        if (S2[Period + 1] == 4)
                            Team = Team4;
            }
            return Team;
        }
        public String TeamxviaTeam3(Int32 Period)
        {
            String Team = "";
            if (S1[Period + 1] == 3)
            {
                if (S2[Period + 1] == 1)
                    Team = Team1;
                else
                    if (S2[Period + 1] == 2)
                        Team = Team2;
                    else
                        if (S2[Period + 1] == 4)
                            Team = Team4;
            }
            return Team;
        }
        public String TeamxviaTeam4(Int32 Period)
        {
            String Team = "";
            if (S1[Period + 1] == 4)
            {
                if (S2[Period + 1] == 1)
                    Team = Team1;
                else
                    if (S2[Period + 1] == 2)
                        Team = Team2;
                    else
                        if (S2[Period + 1] == 3)
                            Team = Team3;
            }
            return Team;
        }
    }
