## File: ./ProbabilityMach/Regresion.cs
```
﻿/*****************************
 * Ramin Edjlal CopyRigth 2015
 * Regresion of Middle state .
 */ 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class Regresion
    {
        static Regresion Node;
        Regresion F;
        Regresion B;
        public Double[] Score = new Double[2];
        public Double[] LastProbabilty = new Double[2];
        public Double[] HittingGoals = new Double[2];
        public Double[] EatingGoals = new Double[2];
        public Double[] DiffernetGolas = new Double[2];

        public static Int32 GamePeriod = 0;

        static public Double[,] ScoreA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] LastProbabiltyA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] HittingGoalsA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] EatingGoalsA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] DiffernetGolasA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public String[] TeamA = new String[32];

        static Int32 Index = 0;

        const Double ScoreCo = 0.01;
        const Double DifferetGoalsCo = 0.01;
        const Double LastProbabiltyCo = 0.01;
        const Double HittingGoalsCo = 0.01;
        const Double EatingGoalsCo = -0.01;

        public String Team1 = "";
        public String Team2 = "";

        static public void LoadTeam(String filePath)
        {
            filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."));
            filePath = filePath + "1.txt";
            Int32 J = 0;
            for (Int32 i = 2; i <= 9; i++)
            {
                String A = (new LoadSave()).Load(filePath);

                String B = A.Substring(1, A.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                B = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                B = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                B = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf(".") - 1);
                filePath = filePath + i.ToString() + ".txt";
            }
        }

        public void SetGamePeriod(Int32 gameperiod)
        {
            GamePeriod = gameperiod;
        }

        public Int32 IndexOF(String team)
        {
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 32; i++)
                if (team == TeamA[i])
                    return i;
            return -1;
        }
        public Int32 FirstTeamWin()
        {
            ScoreA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = Score[0];
            LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = LastProbabilty[0];
            HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = HittingGoals[0];
            EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = EatingGoals[0];
            DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = DiffernetGolas[0];

            ScoreA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = Score[1];
            LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = LastProbabilty[1];
            HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = HittingGoals[1];
            EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = EatingGoals[1];
            DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = DiffernetGolas[1];

            Double TeamAll1 = 0, TeamAll2 = 0;
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < GamePeriod; i++)
            {
                TeamAll1 = TeamAll1 + (ScoreA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * ScoreCo +
                   DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * DifferetGoalsCo +
                    LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * LastProbabiltyCo +
                    HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * HittingGoalsCo +
                    EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * EatingGoalsCo);
                TeamAll2 = TeamAll2 + (ScoreA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * ScoreCo +
                                   DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * DifferetGoalsCo +
                                    LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * LastProbabiltyCo +
                                    HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * HittingGoalsCo +
                                    EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * EatingGoalsCo);


            }
            Double x1 = TeamAll1 / (TeamAll1 + TeamAll2);
            Double x2 = TeamAll2 / (TeamAll1 + TeamAll2);
            if ((x1 - x2) < -0.66666666666666666666)
                return -1;
            else
                if ((x1 - x2) > 0.66666666666666666666)
                    return 1;
            return 0;
        }
        static public void Reset()
        {
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 32; i++)
                for (Int32 j = 0; j < 6; j++)
                {
                    ScoreA[i, j] = 0;
                    DiffernetGolasA[i, j] = 0;
                    LastProbabiltyA[i, j] = 0;
                    HittingGoalsA[i, j] = 0;
                    EatingGoalsA[i, j] = 0;
                }
        }
        Boolean LessThan(String A, String B)
        {
            Int32 ii = A.Length;
            if (ii > B.Length)
                ii = B.Length;
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < ii; i++)
            {
                if (A[i] == B[i])
                    continue;
                else
                    if (A[i] < B[i])
                        return true;
                    else
                        return false;
            }
            return false;
        }
        public void Sort(ref Regresion reg)
        {
            Double[] ScoreA = new Double[4];
            Double[] EatA = new Double[4];
            Double[] HitA = new Double[4];
            Double[] ProA = new Double[4];
            String[] StrA = new String[4];

            Int32[] Index = new Int32[4];
            Index[0] = 1;
            Index[1] = 2;
            Index[2] = 3;
            Index[3] = 4;
            ScoreA[0] = this.Score[0];
            ScoreA[1] = this.Score[1];
            ScoreA[2] = reg.Score[0];
            ScoreA[3] = reg.Score[1];

            EatA[0] = this.EatingGoals[0];
            EatA[1] = this.EatingGoals[1];
            EatA[2] = reg.EatingGoals[0];
            EatA[3] = reg.EatingGoals[1];

            HitA[0] = this.HittingGoals[0];
            HitA[1] = this.HittingGoals[1];
            HitA[2] = reg.HittingGoals[0];
            HitA[3] = reg.HittingGoals[1];

            ProA[0] = this.LastProbabilty[0];
            ProA[1] = this.LastProbabilty[1];
            ProA[2] = reg.LastProbabilty[0];
            ProA[3] = reg.LastProbabilty[1];

            StrA[0] = this.Team1;
            StrA[1] = this.Team2;
            StrA[2] = reg.Team1;
            StrA[3] = reg.Team2;

            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                for (Int32 j = i + 1; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (LessThan(StrA[i], StrA[j]))
                    {
                        Double S = ScoreA[i];
                        Int32 SS = Index[i];
                        ScoreA[i] = ScoreA[j];
                        Index[i] = Index[j];
                        ScoreA[j] = S;
                        Index[j] = SS;

                        S = EatA[i];
                        EatA[i] = EatA[j];
                        EatA[j] = S;

                        S = HitA[i];
                        HitA[i] = HitA[j];
                        HitA[j] = S;

                        S = ProA[i];
                        ProA[i] = ProA[j];
                        ProA[j] = S;

                        String SSS = StrA[i];
                        StrA[i] = StrA[j];
                        StrA[j] = SSS;

                    }
                }

            this.Score[0] = ScoreA[0];
            this.Score[1] = ScoreA[1];
            reg.Score[0] = ScoreA[2];
            reg.Score[1] = ScoreA[3];

            this.EatingGoals[0] = EatA[0];
            this.EatingGoals[1] = EatA[1];
            reg.EatingGoals[0] = EatA[2];
            reg.EatingGoals[1] = EatA[3];

            this.HittingGoals[1] = HitA[1];
            reg.HittingGoals[0] = HitA[2];
            reg.HittingGoals[1] = HitA[3];

## File: ./Regresion.cs
```
﻿/*****************************
 * Ramin Edjlal CopyRigth 2015
 * Regresion of Middle state .
 */ 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

namespace ProbabilityMach
{
    class Regresion
    {
        static Regresion Node;
        Regresion F;
        Regresion B;
        public Double[] Score = new Double[2];
        public Double[] LastProbabilty = new Double[2];
        public Double[] HittingGoals = new Double[2];
        public Double[] EatingGoals = new Double[2];
        public Double[] DiffernetGolas = new Double[2];

        public static Int32 GamePeriod = 0;

        static public Double[,] ScoreA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] LastProbabiltyA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] HittingGoalsA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] EatingGoalsA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public Double[,] DiffernetGolasA = new Double[32, 6];
        static public String[] TeamA = new String[32];

        static Int32 Index = 0;

        const Double ScoreCo = 0.01;
        const Double DifferetGoalsCo = 0.01;
        const Double LastProbabiltyCo = 0.01;
        const Double HittingGoalsCo = 0.01;
        const Double EatingGoalsCo = -0.01;

        public String Team1 = "";
        public String Team2 = "";

        static public void LoadTeam(String filePath)
        {
            filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf("."));
            filePath = filePath + "1.txt";
            Int32 J = 0;
            for (Int32 i = 2; i <= 9; i++)
            {
                String A = (new LoadSave()).Load(filePath);

                String B = A.Substring(1, A.IndexOf(":") - 1);
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                B = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                B = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                B = A.Substring(0, A.IndexOf(":"));
                A = A.Remove(0, A.IndexOf(":") + 1);
                TeamA[J] = B;
                J++;

                filePath = filePath.Remove(filePath.IndexOf(".") - 1);
                filePath = filePath + i.ToString() + ".txt";
            }
        }

        public void SetGamePeriod(Int32 gameperiod)
        {
            GamePeriod = gameperiod;
        }

        public Int32 IndexOF(String team)
        {
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 32; i++)
                if (team == TeamA[i])
                    return i;
            return -1;
        }
        public Int32 FirstTeamWin()
        {
            ScoreA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = Score[0];
            LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = LastProbabilty[0];
            HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = HittingGoals[0];
            EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = EatingGoals[0];
            DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team1), GamePeriod - 1] = DiffernetGolas[0];

            ScoreA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = Score[1];
            LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = LastProbabilty[1];
            HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = HittingGoals[1];
            EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = EatingGoals[1];
            DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team2), GamePeriod - 1] = DiffernetGolas[1];

            Double TeamAll1 = 0, TeamAll2 = 0;
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < GamePeriod; i++)
            {
                TeamAll1 = TeamAll1 + (ScoreA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * ScoreCo +
                   DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * DifferetGoalsCo +
                    LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * LastProbabiltyCo +
                    HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * HittingGoalsCo +
                    EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team1), i] * EatingGoalsCo);
                TeamAll2 = TeamAll2 + (ScoreA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * ScoreCo +
                                   DiffernetGolasA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * DifferetGoalsCo +
                                    LastProbabiltyA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * LastProbabiltyCo +
                                    HittingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * HittingGoalsCo +
                                    EatingGoalsA[IndexOF(Team2), i] * EatingGoalsCo);


            }
            Double x1 = TeamAll1 / (TeamAll1 + TeamAll2);
            Double x2 = TeamAll2 / (TeamAll1 + TeamAll2);
            if ((x1 - x2) < -0.66666666666666666666)
                return -1;
            else
                if ((x1 - x2) > 0.66666666666666666666)
                    return 1;
            return 0;
        }
        static public void Reset()
        {
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 32; i++)
                for (Int32 j = 0; j < 6; j++)
                {
                    ScoreA[i, j] = 0;
                    DiffernetGolasA[i, j] = 0;
                    LastProbabiltyA[i, j] = 0;
                    HittingGoalsA[i, j] = 0;
                    EatingGoalsA[i, j] = 0;
                }
        }
        Boolean LessThan(String A, String B)
        {
            Int32 ii = A.Length;
            if (ii > B.Length)
                ii = B.Length;
            for (Int32 i = 0; i < ii; i++)
            {
                if (A[i] == B[i])
                    continue;
                else
                    if (A[i] < B[i])
                        return true;
                    else
                        return false;
            }
            return false;
        }
        public void Sort(ref Regresion reg)
        {
            Double[] ScoreA = new Double[4];
            Double[] EatA = new Double[4];
            Double[] HitA = new Double[4];
            Double[] ProA = new Double[4];
            String[] StrA = new String[4];

            Int32[] Index = new Int32[4];
            Index[0] = 1;
            Index[1] = 2;
            Index[2] = 3;
            Index[3] = 4;
            ScoreA[0] = this.Score[0];
            ScoreA[1] = this.Score[1];
            ScoreA[2] = reg.Score[0];
            ScoreA[3] = reg.Score[1];

            EatA[0] = this.EatingGoals[0];
            EatA[1] = this.EatingGoals[1];
            EatA[2] = reg.EatingGoals[0];
            EatA[3] = reg.EatingGoals[1];

            HitA[0] = this.HittingGoals[0];
            HitA[1] = this.HittingGoals[1];
            HitA[2] = reg.HittingGoals[0];
            HitA[3] = reg.HittingGoals[1];

            ProA[0] = this.LastProbabilty[0];
            ProA[1] = this.LastProbabilty[1];
            ProA[2] = reg.LastProbabilty[0];
            ProA[3] = reg.LastProbabilty[1];

            StrA[0] = this.Team1;
            StrA[1] = this.Team2;
            StrA[2] = reg.Team1;
            StrA[3] = reg.Team2;

            for (Int32 i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                for (Int32 j = i + 1; j < 4; j++)
                {
                    if (LessThan(StrA[i], StrA[j]))
                    {
                        Double S = ScoreA[i];
                        Int32 SS = Index[i];
                        ScoreA[i] = ScoreA[j];
                        Index[i] = Index[j];
                        ScoreA[j] = S;
                        Index[j] = SS;

                        S = EatA[i];
                        EatA[i] = EatA[j];
                        EatA[j] = S;

                        S = HitA[i];
                        HitA[i] = HitA[j];
                        HitA[j] = S;

                        S = ProA[i];
                        ProA[i] = ProA[j];
                        ProA[j] = S;

                        String SSS = StrA[i];
                        StrA[i] = StrA[j];
                        StrA[j] = SSS;

                    }
                }

            this.Score[0] = ScoreA[0];
            this.Score[1] = ScoreA[1];
            reg.Score[0] = ScoreA[2];
            reg.Score[1] = ScoreA[3];

            this.EatingGoals[0] = EatA[0];
            this.EatingGoals[1] = EatA[1];
            reg.EatingGoals[0] = EatA[2];
            reg.EatingGoals[1] = EatA[3];

            this.HittingGoals[0] = HitA[0];
            this.HittingGoals[1] = HitA[1];
            reg.HittingGoals[0] = HitA[2];
            reg.HittingGoals[1] = HitA[3];

            this.LastProbabilty[0] = ProA[0];
            this.LastProbabilty[1] = ProA[1];
            reg.LastProbabilty[0] = ProA[2];
            reg.LastProbabilty[1] = ProA[3];

            this.Team1 = StrA[0];
            this.Team2 = StrA[1];
            reg.Team1 = StrA[2];
            reg.Team2 = StrA[3];

        }
    }
}

```
